# v2026.9.7

AI agents and tools can read these release notes as [plain Markdown](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/openclaw/openclaw/main/CHANGELOG/2026.9.7.md).

OpenClaw 2026.9.7 is snappier under load and smoother in long conversations, with better update backups and rollback protection, plus fixes for upgrading from 2026.9.5. It also adds OpenAI’s [Agents API](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes) and [Sign in with ChatGPT (Beta)](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/authentication), alongside better chat on Mac, iPhone and iPad, and help picking up work after a restart.

**Release scale:** 2,818 pull requests, 518 direct commits, and 344 contributors.

## Installation and Onboarding

Setup reaches the first question sooner and gives clearer guidance when sign-in, pairing, or installation needs attention.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="First-run setup">

[Onboarding](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/onboard) starts sooner and explains invalid options and workspace paths more clearly. If you want a workspace without generated starter files, `openclaw setup --baseline --skip-bootstrap` now remembers that choice and preserves existing files.

If a shared configuration file sets the opposite choice, edit that file directly. For remote setup, omit an unused `--workspace` path if it is invalid on the computer running setup.

In browser setup, arriving suggestions no longer interrupt your typing. Reloading first-run Model Setup returns to its pending question while the same Gateway stays running, but unsubmitted answers are lost. Cancelling the attempt frees the provider choices once cancellation is confirmed.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Load only the selected guided, classic, or unattended setup flow [#160169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160169).
- Shorten the animated banner before the first setup prompt [#160250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160250).
- Use one authenticated reachability check when unattended setup explicitly skips daemon installation, retaining health checks for a running Gateway [#160168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160168).

**Bug fixes**

- Honor and remember baseline skip-bootstrap choices while preserving existing files and shared configuration includes [275d0d4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/275d0d497bf27daf0c908597522b5f7ce4827842). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Preserve numbers, booleans, objects, and null in plugin setup choices without losing neighboring numeric-keyed settings [c762b23](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c762b23736449d9de045d052acaba26bcd3e21e7). Thanks @moerai.
- Keep temporary model-verification runs out of ordinary chat and session events [#155777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155777). Thanks @mertbasar0, @obviyus, and @axiom-ncis.
- Open and focus the destination chat after an Ask OpenClaw handoff, without transferring the conversation or starting work [#155907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155907). Thanks @romneyda.
- Report unknown or incomplete root options instead of opening setup or terminal chat [#159213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159213).
- Skip startup polling when interactive service installation is declined and no temporary Gateway exists; managed starts and explicit Gateway reuse retain their waits [#160307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160307).
- Reject unusable workspace paths before reset or setup, and identify workspace or Gateway failures without blaming successful AI access [#160373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160373).
- Keep focus on the composer or another active control when onboarding suggestions arrive [#160677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160677).
- Label skipped AI setup as Local setup, reserving Inference ready for a verified model connection [#160713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160713).
- Restore the pending first-run model question after a browser reload on the same running Gateway and release provider choices after confirmed cancellation; unsubmitted answers are not saved, and Gateway restarts retain guarded recovery [#160774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160774).

**Documentation**

- Clarify setup prerequisites, caller-supplied example inputs, and links to pairing, memory-provider, and recovery instructions [4c9869c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4c9869c6fd012b94123f0cae4554e1d605c5c846). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Show `setup --baseline` in the CLI help example for unattended baseline setup [#159214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159214).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Model account sign-in">

First-run setup shows detected API keys and tokens again, with clearer sign-in choices. **Connect & verify** checks your credentials and can select the agent's model. Device-code sign-in no longer ends early because of OpenClaw's shorter timer, though the provider's code can still expire. Custom model addresses must use HTTP or HTTPS, and automated [LM Studio setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/lmstudio) now explains connection and model-check failures.

You can restart your abandoned sign-in for the same provider and setup target using the same sign-in method. Use the newest sign-in link. Model verification also stops on connection failures without the repeated retries used in ordinary chat, so retry setup yourself after a temporary outage clears.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep setup activation alive through the provider's device-code sign-in window [#156440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156440).
- Restore first-run provider cards, detected manual credentials, and distinct login choices [#159412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159412). Thanks @stevenlee-oai.
- Return structured LM Studio setup failures with recovery guidance, a nonzero exit status, and no application of the proposed provider settings [#160196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160196).
- Reject malformed or non-HTTP(S) custom model addresses before interactive credential selection and verification [#160301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160301).
- Replace an abandoned matching provider sign-in for the same owner and setup target, accepting only the latest retry and rejecting old callbacks; protected preparation and credential-saving work remain busy [#160574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160574). Thanks @stevenlee-oai.
- Stop setup verification on refused connections or unreachable names and networks, with recovery guidance for configured provider URLs; routes without an explicit provider base URL may still report a timeout [#160813](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160813).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Device pairing and permissions">

OpenClaw can use your configured public HTTPS address for [device join links and QR setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/devices). Used or expired dashboard links now explain how to get a fresh one with `openclaw dashboard` on the computer running OpenClaw. Links remain single-use and expire after ten minutes. When a browser requests admin access, its Inbox now shows the exact approval command to run on the computer hosting OpenClaw.

Browser access also uses that public address when no allowed-address list is set, including in existing setups. Existing lists still take precedence. Before removing a list, check any other browser addresses you need. If you run OpenClaw in a container, check that its localhost dashboard still opens.

On Android, **Request all** lets you review optional permissions together and finish setup even if you deny SMS access. Camera and location still have separate on/off switches, and notification-listener access needs a separate Settings step. SMS requires a supported third-party build, a phone-capable device, and Gateway approval. Google Play builds do not support SMS.

Shared networks have more room for simultaneous connection attempts, with the default allowance rising from 32 to 128 pending, unauthenticated connections per IP. That permits more sockets to consume server resources; administrators can [lower the allowance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security/rate-limiting#unauthenticated-websocket-connections). A retry countdown shows when the browser will try again after OpenClaw answers an HTTP check. The busy message does not diagnose the failed connection, so persistent problems still need [connection troubleshooting](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Inherit `gateway.publicOrigin` for browser access only when `allowedOrigins` is omitted; explicit lists, including an empty list, override inheritance without disabling other local-origin exceptions, and public-origin changes revoke browser connections no longer allowed [#157170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157170).
- Request eligible Android permissions together without automatically enabling camera or location, and allow setup to finish after SMS denial [#159727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159727). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Show commands to reconnect a previously paired node when `openclaw connect` has no target; the user still runs the commands, and stale pairing needs fresh enrollment [#159810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159810).

**Bug fixes**

- Use the configured public address as the loopback pairing fallback while preserving explicit, remote, Tailscale, and LAN routes; remote QR setup retains its separate prerequisites [#159465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159465).
- Describe rejected Android and Apple setup codes as no longer valid, with fresh-code guidance instead of assuming expiration [#159745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159745). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Distinguish partial SMS read/send access and explain when Android restrictions may prevent granting the missing permission [#159747](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159747). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Preserve pending node pairing requests when unchanged permission keys arrive in a different order [#159759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159759).
- Show denied users administrator role guidance instead of describing a reachable Gateway as unavailable [#159948](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159948).
- Clear rejected dashboard pairing tokens before manual-credential retries and explain fresh-link recovery, including `openclaw dashboard --json` when browser or clipboard access is unavailable [#160684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160684).
- Show `openclaw devices approve <requestId>` for a pending browser admin-access request, retaining existing approval rules [#160685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160685).
- Raise the default pending unauthenticated connection allowance per resolved IP from 32 to 128 and show existing retry timing after a verified same-origin HTTP liveness check; restore the old allowance with `OPENCLAW_MAX_PREAUTH_CONNECTIONS_PER_IP=32` and restart the Gateway [#160814](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160814).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Channel setup and settings">

You can ask setup chat to remove a setting override and return to the inherited default without changing neighboring settings. Existing approvals still apply. Remote rescue messages cannot make this change, so use your regular agent or the local CLI. Automated [channel setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/channels) also points to options your selected channel supports when something goes wrong.

After dismissing a completed channel setup, you can start the next without the previous dialog leaving setup occupied. Channel cards refresh automatically once settings are applied, so you do not need to refresh the page to see the updated status.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Give channel-specific help when noninteractive setup cannot use `--use-env`, while keeping guided setup's interactive-terminal requirement [#157551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157551). Thanks @sxh313 and @obviyus.
- Let the Gateway handle other work while channel setup checks policy after selecting an agent, with configuration ownership rechecked before proceeding [#158783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158783).
- Remove approved configuration overrides through setup or delegated chat, preserve sibling settings, and refuse remote rescue deletion [#158873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158873). Thanks @vacinc.
- Close the completed channel wizard before starting another and wait for pending cancellation without aborting committed setup work [#160705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160705).
- Refresh channel cards after configuration is accepted and prevent slow probes from replacing newer status [#160853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160853).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Installation and platform support">

Fresh npm installs include a missing dependency that could prevent OpenClaw from starting. Existing services using a supported Bun version keep it during reinstalls and updates unless you have explicitly selected another runtime. The [Bun compatibility guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/bun-compatibility) also covers installation on Bun-only machines. Node remains the default for fresh installs when available.

On Windows, the installer reports failure if the terminal still cannot find OpenClaw. Open a new terminal and run `openclaw doctor` as advised. For Windows source checkouts running under Bun, a startup fix lets OpenClaw open its databases and save device tokens. The [FreeBSD installation guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/installer) explains the missing native Codex binary and the OpenAI API alternative, which requires an API key and explicit OpenClaw runtime selection. API usage is billed separately from a ChatGPT or Codex subscription. Administrators can also follow a new [team server deployment guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/team-server).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Report an unusable Windows PATH as an installation failure, preserving catchable PowerShell scriptblock errors and recovery guidance [#134406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134406). Thanks @ly85206559 and @fuller-stack-dev.
- Select source-worker loaders from the running runtime's identity when reusing a renamed Bun or Node executable [#158521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158521).
- Bundle the required Undici dependency alongside patched Proxyline so fresh npm installs and staged update candidates can start [#160015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160015).
- Retain supported recorded Bun service runtimes during reinstalls and update refreshes, with an automatic Bun-only first-install fallback [#160189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160189).
- Open SQLite stores and save device tokens when running TypeScript source under Bun on Windows, without adding Node’s unnecessary loader; built installations were unaffected [#160310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160310).

**Documentation**

- Explain FreeBSD's native Codex limitation, explicit API runtime selection, and separate API billing [#155980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155980). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.
- Add a Linux team-server walkthrough covering Cloudflare sign-in, identities, roles, public session links, backups, and verification [#157085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157085).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Building from source">

You can build and lint an OpenClaw checkout inside another Node.js project using its own dependencies. Failed builds can be retried without manually removing stale locks, and cancelling `pnpm openclaw` during a rebuild no longer launches the CLI afterward.

The prerelease compiler now supports build hosts on macOS arm64/x64, Linux arm/arm64/x64, and Windows arm64/x64. BSD, AIX, SunOS, and other Linux architectures no longer have compiler packages, so check your build host before upgrading. This restriction concerns building OpenClaw, not running an installed copy.

Local macOS packaging restores the committed Swift dependency versions before preparing each build, avoiding dependency drift when reusing a build cache. If a later step changes a dependency, the error names the changed version.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Finish Linux help generation when owned descendants have exited but await reaping, while still refusing live or unobservable work [#158448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158448). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Explain uncomparable installer versions before replacement and reject linked plugin-build output paths that could redirect writes or cleanup outside `dist` [#159199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159199).
- Release source-build ownership after completed failures and reject invalid build arguments without starting work [#160059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160059).
- Honor cancellation during source rebuilds and lock waits, without launching the CLI or recording cancelled preparation as complete [#160172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160172). Thanks @romneyda, @vyctorbrzezowski, and @jalehman.
- Restore committed Swift dependency pins before creating the local macOS package snapshot and identify later pin changes in errors [#160809](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160809).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Support nested source builds with checkout-owned declaration inputs, using TypeScript `7.1.0-dev.20260917.1` and its narrower native build-host coverage [#158674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158674).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Web UI

Long conversations in the [Control UI](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui) scroll more smoothly and load older messages with fewer pauses, alongside improvements to writing and navigating between chats.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Conversation sidebar">

The sidebar’s [Filter & sort panel](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar) brings filtering and display choices together. Pinned chats stay in Pages across agents, and saved forks stay beneath their parent conversation after a restart. Unnamed heartbeat chats are hidden unless you choose Show system sessions.

- Show agent identity in dashboard conversation tabs [#158547](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158547). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Refresh conversation branches without rescanning ordinary appends [#159309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159309).

<Accordion title="New chat settings">

New chats can start with the selected agent’s [configured model and reasoning defaults](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-gateway), while still letting you choose different settings for a draft. This is optional; remembering your last choice remains the default. Update the Gateway and UI together, then refresh or reconnect before enabling it.

Guests with [session-write access](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/operator-scopes) can create and control their own chats. Full permission mode, sandbox changes, and changes to an existing chat’s context window still require an administrator.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

**Improvements**

- Start fresh sessions from configured agent defaults [#156866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156866). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Avoid repeated history rendering during session startup [#159562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159562).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve the chosen runtime in Auto and new-worktree sessions, and distinguish retained drafts from already-created sessions in recovery errors [#160547](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160547). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Preserve chat settings during session creation [#154807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154807).
- Enable permitted Guest session creation, settings and Stop controls [#155565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155565). Thanks @shakkernerd.

<Accordion title="Long conversations and tables">

Long conversations have fewer pauses when you type, scroll, or load older messages, and preserve your reading position as content arrives. [Chat tables](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) wrap more clearly, with Copy and Expand below the content. Resumed work keeps consecutive tool activity in one expandable log.

Block-streamed replies keep table headers and rows together when the table fits the configured streaming size limit. Larger tables can still split, and individual channels retain their own message limits.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Live reply streaming">

Live replies use less network traffic by sending new text instead of repeatedly sending the whole answer. Updated bundled clients assemble the reply as it arrives.

Third-party raw clients must adopt the [updated streaming contract](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/rpc-bootstrap-and-events), even though the protocol still reports version 4. Custom SDK transports also need the documented event-ordering changes. Older clients can lose reply text, and reconnect recovery cannot always restore a complete answer.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Send incremental live text and require raw-client reconstruction and reconnect migration [#158587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158587).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Conversation recovery">

Split panes restore their own conversations after reload, and chats follow history changes made in another tab while keeping unsent drafts. Quoted reply drafts also retain their selected quote. Removed queued messages stay hidden after reconnect, though their stored content is not erased.

If OpenClaw warns that a stopped reply could not be saved, [copy its visible text before leaving](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Reduce work in archived conversation searches [#156665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156665).
- Hide the empty outbox notice while drafting offline [#156914](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156914). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.

- Reduce repeated byte-counting during chat-history catch-up [#158469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158469).
- Stagger automatic Gateway reconnection attempts [#158536](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158536).
- Keep session lists responsive during large history reads [#159328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159328).

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Background chat previews">

Sidebar previews send fewer updates while your open chat keeps its full live reply stream. Hidden browser tabs also stop receiving sidebar-only activity. If you use a custom SDK client, [update the Gateway alongside the SDK](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/rpc-bootstrap-and-events) to keep live subscriptions working.

- Reuse automation reads and CPU identity; CPU topology changes require a restart [#155686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155686). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce Control UI work during session updates and streaming [#156196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156196).

<Accordion title="Steering and Stop controls">

You can [steer active work](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue-steering) even when an older message is waiting as a follow-up. People with the same permissions can also steer shared turns, including across devices using the same shared Owner token. Steering does not interrupt running tools and remains unavailable for Incognito and cloud-worker turns. A ready follow-up replaces Stop with Send, Queue, or Steer; clear the draft to bring Stop back. Stop remains available for drafts that cannot send, except during active dictation.

In shared turns, specify whose [screen](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/screen) or [theme](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/theme) should change. Other personal settings need a new personal turn. The original owner’s permissions, approvals, and provider account still apply.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Let equal-permission participants steer shared turns [#159850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159850).

- Restore contrast for mobile composer action icons [#155951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155951). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

- Preserve consecutive pending chat message order [#160240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160240). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Recover Stop targeting when chat retains an inactive run [#160596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160596). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Work summaries and progress cards">

Agent commentary stays beside its tool activity, and work summaries distinguish skipped tools from failures or approval requests. During a run, the activity row keeps a readable tool purpose visible between calls, normally giving you three seconds to read it. Failures, blocks, and completion appear immediately.

[Progress cards](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/progress-card) remember whether you left them open during the current visit. With write access, you can dismiss any card, including unfinished or note-only progress, without stopping the agent; later progress may create another one.

- Keep agent narration inline and avatars top-aligned [#156926](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156926). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @vincentkoc.
- Show filesystem paths in tool details and diagnostics while retaining credential masking [#157059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157059).
- Show total tool calls alongside failures [#157904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157904). Thanks @romneyda.
- Align chat composer status bar heights [#160016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160016).

**Bug fixes**

- Remove unfinished and note-only progress cards when dismissed, preserving newer agent updates [#160867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160867). Thanks @laurencebrown.
- Preserve progress around questions and avoid false drafts from read-only file searches [#152665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152665). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @vincentkoc.

- Count nested tool operations once in activity summaries [#157438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157438).
- Collapse progress cards after delayed scroll observations [#158153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158153).
- Distinguish skipped tools from blocked or failed activity [#159502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159502).
- Avoid redundant comment-pin layout and show disabled state [#159559](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159559).
- Dismiss saved progress in every card state [#160126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160126).

<Accordion title="Goal controls">

[Goal controls](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/goal) stay with the right conversation while it opens and explain rejected changes or failed outcome checks. Starting a new Goal also keeps older failed messages separate, with their own Retry action.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Personal instructions">

On shared Gateways with at least two individual profiles, you can edit your [personal instructions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/user-model) in Profile or through authenticated chat. Individual sign-in is required for separate personal files; a shared Owner login edits the shared profile. This works in local agent workspaces, with a 4,000-character limit and existing prompt limits. Single-user setups still use the main `USER.md` file. Save before closing the browser, and avoid editing the same file elsewhere at the same time.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Edit personal instructions in Profile settings or chat [#155256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155256). Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Settings errors and drafts">

When Settings rejects a change, it explains why and keeps your edits available to correct or retry. [Discard draft and reload](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings) restores saved settings and removes **all unsaved configuration edits**. If a save’s result is uncertain, return to the original Gateway to retry or discard it. Follow any Apply or restart instructions before expecting a saved change to take effect.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="File drafts and previews">

If an update blocks the editor, [Review file drafts](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect) lets you copy or download unsaved work. Keep anything you need before explicitly discarding drafts and refreshing. Copying alone does not unlock refresh, and a manual browser reload or tab closure still loses these drafts. Incognito drafts have the same memory-only limit.

File editing also preserves multiline paste and updates Review after saving. Remote workspace files can be previewed, but those previews are read-only.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Speed up agent-file editing with Preview closed [#159902](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159902). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

- Refresh file details and Review diffs after saving workspace edits [#156327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156327).
- Clear hidden drafts after read-only file reloads [#156340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156340). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Protect drafts during competing file creation; unsupported remote providers require an update or host-side creation [#157020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157020).
- Preserve file line endings when pasting multiline text [#157998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157998).
- Preserve unsent Incognito drafts during automatic updates [#158213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158213).

- Avoid no-changes claims for incomplete Review results [#159529](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159529).

**Documentation**

- Correct Files panel navigation guidance [#130596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130596).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Move file-edit planning to workers; unavailable planning fails before writes and active rename scans may delay quota detection [#158443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158443).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="PDFs and image attachments">

[PDF attachments](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) hosted by your OpenClaw instance can open beside chat, keeping your page and zoom when their link renews. Preview supports files up to 16 MiB; external PDFs, Office files, and unavailable previews use downloads. Oversized static PNGs up to 25 megapixels can be [resized locally](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/webchat) for upload while keeping transparency. Oversized animated PNGs are rejected rather than converted to a still image.

Ordinary chat and new-chat submissions now check the combined attachment size before sending and keep your draft if it is too large. PNGs being resized can reserve more space than they eventually need, so try a smaller batch if they are rejected. Large pasted-text cards also have a visible X to remove an accidental paste without disturbing the rest of your draft.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Remove pasted-text attachments directly from their composer cards [#160834](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160834). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Read PDF attachments beside your conversation [#148399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148399). Thanks @jjjhenriksen, @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Move between expanded videos in one chat turn [#154051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154051). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Defer offscreen chat image requests [#155370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155370). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Load offscreen file-card details on demand without losing keyboard focus [#155623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155623). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Avoid unnecessary parsing when listing conversation images [#158371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158371).
- Move large-upload preparation to workers and honor cancellation; automatic overload retry remains unsupported [#158420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158420).
- Share audio/video inspections and preserve outgoing attachments under load [#158548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158548).
- Prepare large artifact downloads outside the Gateway thread [#158583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158583).
- Speed up repeated artifact lists for unchanged sessions [#159537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159537).
- Resize oversized static PNG attachments in chat [#159960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159960). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Check combined attachment sizes and preserve ordinary drafts; recovered startup and pending-placement New Session submissions remain excluded [#160071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160071).
- Preserve protected egress for attachments with international filenames [#156166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156166). Thanks @siri1410, @obviyus, @lisheguo.
- Preserve original attachment download names [#156588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156588).
- Restore pasted-text previews before sending [#157390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157390).
- Keep startup file cards separate from the text bubble [#158700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158700). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Restore reply previews for media-only assistant messages [#158701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158701). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Preserve uploaded image filenames in chat history [#159216](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159216).
- Reuse loaded chat images when scrolling back [#159486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159486). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Camera preview">

Take photo opens a [local camera preview](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui) so you can capture, retake, and confirm a photo before attaching it. Confirmation adds it to your draft without sending it. Live preview needs HTTPS or localhost and camera permission; if permission is denied, choose Retry or Upload photo.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Preview, capture and confirm camera photos; permission denial offers retry or upload rather than native capture [#160253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160253).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Copying messages and opening links">

You can copy your prompts as Markdown, and conversation exports skip empty speaker sections. The optional [Open links outside OpenClaw setting](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings) now also applies to browser-preview cards. It is off by default and saved per Gateway in the current browser or app, without syncing across devices.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Give GitHub references neutral theme backgrounds while retaining status colors and keyboard focus [#160844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160844). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Copy user messages as Markdown [#156563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156563). Thanks @vacinc.
- Choose to open links outside OpenClaw [#159887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159887). Thanks @vacinc.
- Keep GitHub item identity in failed link previews [#160219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160219). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Improve GitHub reference contrast, focus, and wrapping in chat [72b76842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/72b76842012f405400a2b6b694f46bd62b7bca1c). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Preserve typing focus while GitHub documents load [#157202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157202).
- Omit empty sections from conversation exports [#157590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157590).
- Preserve repository context in GitHub folder-link labels [#157619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157619). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Use neutral labels for messages without recorded authors [#160257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160257). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Make browser preview cards follow external-opening preferences [#160410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160410). Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Side chat selections and images">

Select a passage and choose Ask in side chat to add an optional, editable comment without replacing your existing draft. The passage and comment share a 16,000-character limit and accompany only the current question, so select the passage again for a later question.

You can drop or paste images into [Side chat](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) for the current question. Choose an image-capable utility model, then use Retry if the previous model could not read the image. Images are not saved in restored history, so reattach them for later questions. Compatible CLI setups without direct provider credentials also gain a text-only fallback, without tools or images.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add editable comments to selected passages for the current Side chat question [#160747](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160747). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Ask Side chat about dropped or pasted images [#156840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156840). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Remove automatic summaries from Side chat [#156892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156892). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.

**Bug fixes**

- Explain unsupported Side chat images and preserve Retry [#156900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156900). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Preserve Side chat input during delayed updates [#156990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156990).
- Offer Side chat fallback without direct API credentials [#157474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157474). Thanks @rogeriochaves, @obviyus, @sidboswell.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Model picker and usage">

Large model lists are more responsive to search, and the picker opens to the selected model’s provider. [Usage](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/feature-reference) keeps previous totals visible while refreshing and lets you retry failures. [Doctor](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor) reports incomplete totals; request usage again to retry. If Cmd+K returns no models, check model settings for provider-refresh problems.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Group Decision model choices by provider [#155301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155301). Thanks @jalehman.
- Keep model caches across unrelated metadata updates [#157254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157254).
- Remove routine model-picker hover tooltips [#158697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158697). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Reduce pauses when searching large model lists [#159905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159905). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Show model search results while other categories load [#151022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151022). Thanks @obviyus.
- Bound memory use when refreshing large-session usage totals [#156937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156937). Thanks @conan-scott.
- Keep usage totals visible through background refresh [#157270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157270). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep metadata and model lists available during unrelated session changes [#158576](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158576).
- Align model-picker search text with reading direction [#159937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159937). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Show the selected model when opening the chat model picker [#160132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160132).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Remove catalog-refresh warnings from Cmd+K, including empty results; diagnostics remain on model pages [#160221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160221). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Online people and running chats">

The [Online sidebar](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/presence) distinguishes recent interaction from simply being connected and now includes your own account. Compact rows show each person’s accessible open and running conversations. The expanded list orders Active, Idle, then unknown activity, with people running work first and names alphabetical within each group. Count sorting, the running-only filter, and aggregate totals have been removed. Quiet reading can show as Idle, and Running excludes queued work and work running only in child conversations.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show compact Online rows with fixed presence and running-work ordering [#160140](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160140). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @vacinc.
- Reduce database work for human-mention suggestions [#158481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158481).
- Reduce repeated presence work for concurrent viewers [#158497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158497).
- Reuse prepared assistant identities and prevent stale avatar cache replacement [#158597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158597).
- Reduce unrelated typing-state scans and expire idle groups [#158649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158649).
- Reduce presence traffic when many tabs connect [#158942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158942).
- Distinguish online connectivity from recent person activity [#159493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159493).
- Show open and running conversations beside online people [#160022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160022).
- Reduce presence traffic during browser join bursts [#160150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160150).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep idle dots amber in Online, session groups, Activity, and person cards across light and dark themes [#160759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160759). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @obviyus.
- Align chat mention avatars with names [#155969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155969). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Recover chat viewer presence after a lost acknowledgement [#156255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156255). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Publish client presence after the handshake succeeds [#159554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159554).
- Include your own account in the Online sidebar [#160618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160618). Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Shared draft previews">

Shared chats show [two full draft previews](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) and summarize additional people typing in a compact row. Paused previews fade away, and resumed typing keeps its place. These are temporary previews of unsent text, not saved messages.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Pace collaborator previews and discard obsolete queued drafts [#158552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158552).
- Simplify collaborator draft labels and idle expiry [#159235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159235). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Keep collaborator typing previews compact in busy chats [#159263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159263). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @shakkernerd.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep collaborator drafts visible during typing pauses [#157100](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157100). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Profile access">

[Profile](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/user-model) explains your current access and offers Reconnect after an administrator changes it. Guests can view and refresh their own profile without gaining editing rights or access to the People roster. Some guests still see initials because profile photos have separate permissions.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Explain current connection access in Profile [#156304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156304). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Improve sign-in pages, disabled-button styling, Mac reconnect and voice controls, and Approvals layout [#157251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157251).
- Load saved avatars through workers, retaining the distinct-profile overload limit [#158445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158445).

**Bug fixes**

- Reuse saved avatars and return retryable overload responses; automatic browser retry is not added [#158676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158676).
- Keep long hostnames inside the sign-in card [#159899](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159899). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Show guests their own Control UI profile [#160503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160503). Thanks @shakkernerd.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Unread chats and notifications">

A conversation can gain an unread marker when work finishes after your last read. Inbox mentions and update cards have consistent expand and dismiss controls, and [browser notification settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/notifications) avoid a background-loading delay that could leave them waiting.

Reviewing a failed-delivery alert clears its Inbox entry and badge while keeping the draft for an explicit retry or discard. Check the draft after retrying, since a later failure may not produce a fresh alert; a dismissed alert can also return after a full reload.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Align Inbox notification ages and controls [#156560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156560). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Batch Web Push recipient preparation [#158595](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158595).

**Bug fixes**

- Correct heading size and spacing in empty Inbox tabs [#160724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160724). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Clear reviewed delivery alerts without resending or deleting drafts; reload and failed-retry alert handling retain known limitations [#160822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160822). Thanks @stevenlee-oai.
- Mark newly completed visible sessions unread [#155690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155690). Thanks @hxy91819.
- Keep approval state scoped to the current Gateway and omit undiscovered model setup options [#158032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158032).
- Reschedule mention expiry after clock rollback [#158614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158614).
- Keep chat subscriptions stable during unrelated approval activity [#158633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158633).
- Keep notification settings responsive during background preparation [#159709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159709).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Today overview and Live Activity">

Activity’s [Today overview](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings) follows your filters and counts matching sessions beyond the first page, placing each at its latest activity hour.

Live Activity now records only while its view and browser tab are visible, for up to 100 accessible active sessions. It misses activity while you are elsewhere and starts empty after you leave the page and return. Raw Debug history likewise starts when diagnostics open and disappears when the last diagnostic view closes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid unnecessary Activity rendering outside Live [#156206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156206).
- Reduce Live Activity rendering overhead [#157952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157952).
- Reuse resident recap facts; matching keys in multiple stores can still select the wrong store [#158803](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158803).
- Add a Today overview to Activity sessions [#159611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159611).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Activity recaps across session-list refreshes [8cea9e99](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8cea9e995769d86ec33b36d60bcdf82f5e092b60). Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Publish ready Activity recaps during unrelated refreshes [#154907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154907).
- Generate Activity recaps for visible agent-created chats [#159458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159458).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Capture raw Debug events only while diagnostics are open, with no earlier-event lookback [#158533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158533).
- Capture Live Activity only while visible, without off-page backfill or retention across page visits [#158574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158574).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Working branch and pull request">

[PR previews](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) follow the chat’s working branch, so an incidental PR link no longer creates a banner. Branch and CI previews disappear on detached or default-branch checkouts. Sidebar badges may not appear until you open a chat, and mobile chat now shows the working branch before a PR exists.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated work in pull-request updates [#158302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158302).
- Reuse unchanged worktree branch suggestions [#158399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158399).
- Skip redundant Git ancestry checks for matching branch tips [#159301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159301).

**Bug fixes**

- Recover stalled pull-request status subscriptions [#156310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156310). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Release PR-panel caches when preparing viewers leave [#157776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157776).
- Show the working branch in mobile chat [#160597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160597). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Show branch-owned PR banners only; detached and default checkouts no longer retain them [#158578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158578). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Worker failure recovery">

After a worker fails, you can [continue the same conversation](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-sessions) by sending another message once cleanup and workspace recovery finish. The replacement uses the original device or cloud profile and keeps the history and last saved workspace. The interrupted turn is not replayed, and unsynced files may be lost. Setup failures or unavailable destinations still need the displayed recovery steps; use Restart session to choose another destination.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Continue conversations after recoverable worker failures [a7ded174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a7ded174bd966dcbc78e5eae1c059b65b5ecb80c).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Cloud worker pool">

Administrators can open [Cloud workers → Pool](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/warm-images) in Settings → Connections to see which workers are ready, preparing, or need attention, and how much reserve capacity is occupied. Cleanup can keep a slot occupied after use. Change limits in Profiles.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show cloud worker readiness and occupied capacity in Settings [#157200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157200).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Dashboards, desktops, and widgets">

Returning to a chat can reopen its existing desktop or live browser tab. [Dashboard gallery navigation](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/dashboards) also keeps widget interactions when you return, though a browser reload still clears unsaved state. Self-contained [HTML and SVG widgets](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/show-widget) can now use up to 10 MiB including their wrapper; registered-source widgets and Discord keep their smaller limits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reveal existing desktops and browsers when returning to chat [b8929c45](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b8929c456559af2ea7d1b0d366c4e8df53a13798). Thanks @vincentkoc, Peter Steinberger.
- Allow wrapped HTML and SVG widgets up to 10 MiB while retaining smaller registered-source and Discord limits [#159970](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159970).

**Bug fixes**

- Stop repeated canvas refresh requests when permission is denied, while retaining retries for temporary failures [#160808](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160808).
- Reduce Workboard stalls when loading or refreshing labeled cards [#156150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156150). Thanks @sunlit-deng, @obviyus, @sonofakel.
- Preserve dashboard widget state when returning through the gallery [#156288](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156288).
- Align desktop headers with sidebar controls [#156432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156432). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep HTML widget padding when maximized [#157203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157203).
- Keep session previews available during unchanged metadata updates [#157943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157943).
- Correct Desktop panel fullscreen ownership [#160277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160277).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browser Talk">

Browser Talk can continue through brief connection stalls and asks you to repeat speech if audio was dropped. The in-call voice dropdown has been removed. Set future-call defaults in [Settings → Talk](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/talk/realtime-sessions), or ask the assistant to change the current call’s voice. Native iOS and Android voice controls are unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Open microphone setup while chat history loads; starting Talk still waits for history [#160686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160686).
- Keep Talk calls running through brief stalls and explain dropped speech [#155839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155839). Thanks @marvinthebored, @peetiegonzalez, @obviyus.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Remove the browser in-call voice dropdown; Settings chooses future defaults and the assistant can change an active call [#159441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159441).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Plugin pages and settings">

Installed plugins open directly from command-palette results, and installation status stays visible during catalog refreshes. If Plugins Advanced settings fail to load after connecting, Reload can try again. Opening a disabled plugin’s page does not enable it.

Installed plugin pages now show [account and credential setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/mcp/control-ui), with Connect, Configure, and Edit actions for supported shared MCP sign-ins and web-search or web-fetch credentials. Sign-in requires administrator access, and Connect is disabled when browser sign-in is unavailable. Connected and Configured describe saved setup, not a live service or working-key check.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show supported account sign-ins and declared web-search or web-fetch credentials on installed plugin pages [#160092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160092). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Give official plugin logos consistent white tiles [#158745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158745). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.

**Bug fixes**

- Disable plugin Connect when the Gateway cannot start browser sign-in [#160761](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160761). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Keep plugin installation status open during catalog refresh [14a1f931](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/14a1f931382ba542f8e8008b36354201ec1f73c0). Thanks @joshavant.
- Place plugin hub titles above navigation tabs [#155192](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155192). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Open installed plugin overviews from command palette results [#157405](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157405).
- Keep plugin sidebar selection aligned with navigation [#157717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157717). Thanks @patrick-erichsen, @jalehman.

- Use the OpenAI company icon for Agents API [#158744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158744). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Themes and keyboard controls">

More menus, panels, and embedded apps follow your selected colors. Keyboard users get clearer focus and shortcut hints, with improved access to sidebar, file, and media controls. High-contrast indicators also improve in the affected controls; imported palettes still use their supplied colors.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Improve keyboard access, screen-reader feedback and contrast across the Control UI [#156050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156050).
- Align close and dismiss icons across web interfaces [#159914](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159914). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Soften sidebar, queued-message and conversation edges [#159915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159915). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Align and clarify keyboard shortcut hints [#159925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159925). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Soften artwork behind chat side-panel cards [#160234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160234). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</AccordionGroup>

## Updates and Maintenance

Verified backups cover more agent databases, and automatic database rollback is available for eligible updates that fail before the new version is allowed to start. Check the migration notes below if you use an older installation or a retired feature.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Backups and rollback">

New [verified backups](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/backup) check that every database recorded during capture is present, including discovered custom and moved agent databases. Older archives may have unknown completeness. Make and verify a full backup before updating; a config-only export is not a recovery backup. Automatic [rollback](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery) has limits. Database restoration requires a capture made while OpenClaw was stopped, with the new version never started and no unaccounted later writes. Changed or incompletely captured configuration files may also need manual recovery.

The new updater removes its database snapshots after a verified successful update. Failed or unverified updates retain them and may leave OpenClaw stopped. Check the update reports before manually deleting older snapshots; Doctor only reports them. Snapshot capture needs substantial free disk space and can block an otherwise compatible update. These protections cannot add missing backups to an update already run by an older updater.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Verify that new backups contain every database recorded at capture [#156211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156211) — Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Reduce redundant backup database-discovery work while preserving worktree files [#156465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156465) — Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Avoid per-file durability work for disposable plugin rehearsal copies [#156793](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156793)
- Retain verified database snapshots for eligible pre-start package rollback and expand agent-backup coverage [#158163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158163) — Thanks @blyszcz, @mmartoccia, @joshbunker.
- Clone disposable plugin snapshots where supported while retaining copy fallback [#159427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159427)

- Keep new POSIX package-to-Git builds on destination storage [#155689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155689) — Thanks @vincentkoc.

<Accordion title="Tasks and TaskFlow removal">

Tasks and TaskFlow panels, commands, and APIs are removed. Use [subagent controls](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/tool-reference), ACP sessions, background processes, Cron history, or Lobster pipelines for their respective work. Conversation transcripts, Workboard, and generated-media completion remain available. Plugin authors must migrate from the [removed Task interfaces](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/removed-surfaces); subagent wait and cancel calls now use `runId` and `runIds`. TaskFlow Webhooks have no equivalent HTTP replacement.

After replacing OpenClaw manually, run `openclaw doctor --fix` before starting it. Existing Task and Flow records are preserved, but some Codex assignments need [manual migration](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations). Review plugin permissions after Webhooks repair. If repair disables plugins or reports conflicting IDs, choose the allowed plugins before re-enabling them.

<Accordion title="Tool Search migration">

[Tool Search](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tool-search) now uses separate search, description, and call tools. Its older JavaScript tool, `tool_search_code`, is removed; use [Code Mode](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/code-mode) for JavaScript orchestration. Normal updates migrate the retired settings. After a manual upgrade or deferred repair, run `openclaw doctor --fix` before restarting, or the old settings will prevent startup. Disabled Tool Search stays disabled.

<Accordion title="Upgrading pre-June installations">

Installations older than June 2026 must upgrade through [2026.9.5](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating#upgrading-very-old-versions) before installing the latest version. Make and verify a backup, stop OpenClaw, and follow that guide using the same account, profile, and installation paths. Run `openclaw doctor --fix` in 2026.9.5 and resolve import problems before continuing. Required plugins and channels must be enabled for their imports. If a newer version has already changed your databases, follow the compatible-backup recovery steps before downgrading.

Manual upgrades and custom container entrypoints need a Doctor repair step before startup; official container entrypoints handle it automatically. If repair is deferred, resolve the reported problem and rerun `openclaw update repair`.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Report safe pre-write repair deferrals and stop repeating completed migration warnings; historical Tasks event ordering is superseded by retirement [#155389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155389) — Thanks @johnnyjrizzo, @mattash.
- Preserve and flush legacy workspace setup archives correctly on Windows [#156704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156704)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Move legacy repair to Doctor, with automatic activation in official containers [2a1402cd3e39](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2a1402cd3e3914352820a24961087f1de0c736d9)
- Require the 2026.9.5 bridge for pre-June installations; remove MCP attachments_fetch limit and its history fallback [#156285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156285)
- Migrate retired heartbeat, llm, embeddedPi, embeddedHarness, and sandbox.perSession settings through 2026.9.5 [#156859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156859)
- Retire pre-June channel-state and shared credentials/oauth.json imports with the 2026.9.5 bridge; retain supported auth JSON imports [#157165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157165)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Interrupted package updates">

Supported POSIX npm updates print a [recovery command](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) before replacing OpenClaw. If the updater disappears, that command can repair the package and launcher. Stop other package managers first, then start OpenClaw and check it after recovery. The helper does not restore your data or plugins and does not support Windows, pnpm, or Bun update paths.

Updates now keep a successfully verified installation if only the final report fails. Update work also has no default time limit when `--timeout` is omitted, so stalled work may need cancellation. These changes apply when the new updater is running.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Load update-recovery service code only when needed [5de949fc4620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5de949fc4620b4c62d05d780caa11870dad506ac) — Thanks Peter Steinberger.

<Accordion title="Source installation updates">

[Source updates](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) now rebuild missing or stale program files even when the source is already current. They also check for build locks and conflicting worktrees before stopping OpenClaw. If an older updater gets stuck, use the [manual source-update procedure](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods) from an external terminal. Confirm all associated compiler processes have stopped before releasing a build lock. Rollback may leave OpenClaw stopped when local changes prevent restoring the previous source.

When the installed updater includes this fix, an eligible failed Git update can restore the previous version even without usable branch history. It leaves the checkout detached at the previous commit and the branch at the failed candidate. Follow the [branch repair instructions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) in the rollback report before continuing work on that branch.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce allocation work in native build artifact validation [#156219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156219) — Thanks @jalehman.
- Reduce source-loading work during legacy channel ownership repair [#156238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156238)
- Reduce name-lookup work in Git source-build artifact checks [#157480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157480)
- Reduce reference-staging work during Git update inspection [#157567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157567)
- Generate parent node-command help from available metadata during source builds [#157586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157586)
- Reuse shared JavaScript import scans during candidate-build validation [#158296](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158296)

**Bug fixes**

- Run fresh verification after same-commit Git rebuilds [3813c048eb7b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3813c048eb7bc84874743976e34fb9ea8aa4f0cd) — Thanks @mikywang.
- Protect discovered live Gateways during source rebuilds and re-enable verified Windows tasks on explicit start [d09ffd222826](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d09ffd222826fcd4545431f24056de42373f6e69) — Thanks @donniefi.
- Reject changed configuration before unnecessary Git candidate checks [#155656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155656) — Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Rebuild stale Git runtimes even when the source revision is already current [#156996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156996) — Thanks @donniefi.
- Limit Git update checks to the intended upstream and preserve truthful comparison results [#157432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157432)
- Check npm update ownership at the resolved installation rather than an unrelated shell prefix [#158285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158285) — Thanks @nomisfi.
- Exclude incidental abandoned runtime staging from Git dependency scans while preserving its files [#158338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158338) — Thanks @jalehman.
- Check relevant Git worktree branch reservations before candidate preparation and shutdown [#158446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158446) — Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Check retained source-build locks before stopping the Gateway [#158849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158849) — Thanks @hannesrudolph.

- Retain source updater dependencies across eligible retired workspace links [#159035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159035) — Thanks @shakkernerd.
- Preserve local work and worktree-held branches during retained Git rollback [#160170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160170)
- Retain build ownership when recording uncertain compiler cleanup fails [#160176](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160176) — Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Restore eligible Git updates on a detached checkout when branch history is unavailable, with guarded branch repair guidance [#160828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160828)

**Documentation**

**Limits and compatibility**

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Bun and Node updates">

Bun installations running the new updater can update, repair, and run Doctor without adding Node, while keeping an explicitly selected Bun runtime. Follow the [Bun setup guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/bun-compatibility) for a first installation. Updaters through 2026.9.6 still need the documented workaround for their first Bun-only upgrade. Run Doctor and repair from the installation used by your Gateway.

On macOS and Linux, Bun headless nodes running the fixed updater can prepare later automatic updates without Node or npm. Windows still requires npm. Bun release discovery and OpenClaw package downloads use public npm even when npm is configured with a private mirror. If your installation requires that mirror, disable `nodeHost.autoUpdate.enabled` and use your normal update process. Node-based services can also recover from a missing Node executable by selecting a supported replacement; custom wrappers need their own repair.

Headless nodes using the default plugins can now activate a prepared update when idle. Nodes running published 2026.9.6 need one manual `openclaw update` followed by `openclaw node restart` to receive this repair. For foreground nodes, stop and restart `openclaw node run` instead. Follow the [node update recovery guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/update-troubleshooting); running work, plugins that cannot report whether they are idle, and database compatibility checks can still defer automatic activation.

<Accordion title="Plugin installation repair">

Doctor keeps existing plugin files until their replacements are recorded and can finish deferred migrations after installing missing plugins. It can also repair [archive-installed plugin links](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/plugins/install) without the original archive. If an older updater hits the affected `import.meta` error, follow the [disable, update, and re-enable steps](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/update-troubleshooting). After an update, run `openclaw completion --write-state` if you want plugin commands included in shell completion.

After a completed plugin migration, `openclaw doctor --fix` can clear repeated warnings about retained source files by archiving leftover history pointers. The imported conversations stay in place, and the archived files remain recoverable.

<Accordion title="Conversation history recovery">

[Doctor](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/sqlite-maintenance) can recover more saved conversation history, including verified transcripts whose old index is missing. Reimporting a restored index preserves newer conversation names, pins, and history. Stop OpenClaw and make a verified backup before repairing history. Keep recovery manifests with the original files and follow Doctor's instructions when it reports conflicts or unknown deletion history; damaged or missing history is not always recoverable.

<Accordion title="Gateway service recovery">

Service checks distinguish a slow start from a stopped or unverified Gateway, and Linux systemd recovery handles more services left offline after updates. Recheck deep status when startup is still pending. System-wide Linux package updates still require you to restart the service yourself. Standalone [`doctor --fix`](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/recovery) may now start a service for the current installation even if you deliberately stopped it before repair.

On macOS, updates can refresh a recognized old service PATH. This may remove `~/bin`, `~/.nix-profile/bin`, `~/.volta/bin`, `~/.asdf/shims`, and fnm alias directories unless they contain the active runtime. If a CLI tool, MCP server, or skill stops finding a command, give it the command's absolute path. Custom PATH settings remain protected.

<Accordion title="Windows service updates">

Windows updates and Doctor handle Scheduled Tasks more consistently, including graceful restart and recovery from temporary database sharing errors. If a restart result says it is unverified, check the Gateway before assuming it is online. An update stranded by an older updater may still need `openclaw update repair` from the installed version.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Container and OCM updates">

Replacement containers can wait for a previous startup to finish instead of repeatedly failing, though the wait can last up to five minutes. The default [container-worker image](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/session-hosting) is now `node:24.21.0-slim`. Offline nodes must preload it or select a cached supported image, such as `node:24.19.0-slim`. A missing image prevents the worker from starting.

Docker installations using OverlayFS do less preparation and cleanup work on updates run by the fixed updater. This reduces waiting around the update, with no demonstrated reduction in Gateway downtime.

With a compatible job-capable OCM manager, you can request an [update](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) from the Control UI or internal `/update` and follow its progress through a reconnect. OCM handles verification and recovery. Explicit Git targets and unsupported package dev-channel requests are refused.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

- Request and follow compatible OCM-owned update jobs through the Gateway [#158932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158932) — Thanks @shakkernerd.

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve worker environments during no-op Doctor checks and settle reconciliation errors [#156160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156160)
- Preserve verified already-current outcomes and use checked copies on OverlayFS [#157770](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157770) — Thanks @romneyda.
- Wait for a prior container’s startup lease to release or expire [#160266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160266) — Thanks @kimiyu-186.
- Reduce redundant checks and disposable-file syncing during OverlayFS update preparation and cleanup; broader update delays remain under investigation [#160845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160845)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Change the default container-worker image to Node 24.21.0-slim with offline preparation guidance [#159401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159401)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Settings reloads">

Saved settings apply without waiting for a file-change notification, and temporary database contention no longer leaves supported changes waiting for another edit. Changing channel connection settings also preserves unaffected conversation lists and model choices. [Configuration errors](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/configuration/hot-reload) now explain whether a file could not be read, a setting is invalid, or a rejected edit saved nothing.

<Accordion title="Shutdown and restart">

Restarts handle active settings changes and plugin reloads more carefully, fixing several shutdown stalls. Clients waiting for results or approvals can disconnect and reconnect without cancelling the underlying work. These fixes take effect once the new Gateway is running; they cannot change a shutdown already handled by an older version.

- Avoid shutdown wait cycles during plugin reload [#157979](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157979)
- Cancel cooperative model acquisition before final Gateway shutdown waits [#158047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158047)

- Stop optional prewarm admission when the Gateway scheduler closes [#158666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158666) — Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Reset host-wake sampling baselines after recovery completes [#159111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159111)
