Android has a full-width writing field with compact controls underneath. Use **+ → Camera** to add a photo or video to your draft; camera permission is required, plus microphone permission for video. Completed replies stay visible when you collapse the tool activity, with counts of failed or blocked tools.

Android model search supports multiple words and small spelling mistakes in longer words. The Effort gauge follows your adjustments even with Fast mode on, shown by a separate bolt. To check token use and costs, open **Chat actions → Context**; the usage ring has moved out of the writing area.

</AccordionGroup>

## Models and Providers

The optional OpenAI Agents API runs chats in hosted workspaces, while Sign in with ChatGPT (Beta) gives eligible accounts another way to connect. Model and video-generation choices also expand.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Agents API hosted workspaces">

The optional [Agents API runtime](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes) runs chats in a persistent hosted Linux workspace, with web search, OpenClaw tools, and file transfers on supported Linux storage. It requires an OpenAI API key with access to the chosen model. [Self-hosted execution](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-agent-harness/runtime-config) needs your own controller and matching workspace path, and does not support file transfers.

Use this runtime only on a single-user Gateway, with OpenClaw as the sole controller of the session. Start a new or reset chat after changing its instructions, tools, model, credentials, or execution environment. Resetting or deleting the local chat does not delete hosted history or files. Check that returned attachments arrived. Choose another runtime if you need per-turn tool restrictions from prompt hooks, restrictions on native shell/file/web access, or strictly tool-free background calls.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add explicitly selected hosted Agents API conversations, with API-key permissions and remote-history retention [#151176](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151176) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Use OpenClaw tools in hosted Agents API sessions [f80fef13](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f80fef130a97de2129105ed37d7154b55c632450) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Transfer hosted Agents API files with Linux-native or Docker-managed state; Windows and macOS bind-mounted state remain unsupported [b5e55716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b5e5571675c910b3ee0f60e83b5192b1e37c6c04) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Show Agents API activity and preserve native tool history [#156182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156182) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Enable live web search in new Agents API sessions [#156306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156306) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Offer self-hosted Agents API execution with an external controller, matching workspace, and a shared 60-second submission deadline [#158307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158307) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Generate dream narratives and restricted completions; a lost creation response can prevent remote session cleanup [#159246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159246) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.

**Bug fixes**

- Supply workspace rules and persona to new Agents API sessions [099220cc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/099220cc937ad0b3ee24fee60cfd45e24e091e41) — Thanks @sjf.
- Report Agents API token usage from settled native turns [#156092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156092) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Honor model-supported Agents API reasoning on new and reused sessions [#156116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156116) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Preserve conversation context in Agents API messages [#156167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156167) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Retry Agents API submissions after temporary server errors [#158775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158775) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Deliver plugin prompt context on ordinary Agents API turns, without enforcing hook tool lists [#159375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159375) — Thanks @sjf-oa, @sjf.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Sign in with ChatGPT (Beta) and OpenAI accounts">

OpenAI setup makes Codex login, Sign in with ChatGPT (Beta), and API keys easier to distinguish, with model choices tied to the selected account. ChatGPT sign-in requires registration and consent to share tokens. It does not cover every model or media tool, and OpenClaw cannot show its quota or set per-app limits. The [authentication comparison](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/authentication) explains which credentials you need.

Signing back into the same verified account preserves conversation choices. Missing older account selections may still need reconnection or an explicit choice. ChatGPT Beta sign-in supports automatic compaction in native Codex, but not manual compaction or remote execution; see the [setup guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/setup).

<Accordion title="GPT-6 Sol and Luna">

GPT-6 Sol and Luna appear for accounts with access through supported OpenAI API, ChatGPT, and native Codex connections. Discovery fixes help subscription accounts find them without changing existing model choices. To use OpenClaw's own runtime, select it explicitly in the [runtime settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes); choosing the model alone does not switch runtimes.

- Restore eligible GPT-6 Sol and Luna subscription discovery and native Codex execution [#155989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155989) — Thanks @fduch-stranger.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Claude Opus 5.5">

[Claude Opus 5.5](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic) becomes the default for new Anthropic and Claude CLI setups and the rolling `opus` choice. Explicit Opus 5 selections stay unchanged. Opus 5.5 always uses thinking, even when you hide it. Automatic PDF analysis can use 5.5 independently of your chat model; set a PDF model explicitly to keep a particular version.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add Claude Opus 5.5 with its thinking and pricing rules [#155966](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155966)
- Use Opus 5.5 for rolling defaults while preserving explicit Claude selections [#156093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156093) — Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.

**Bug fixes**

- Restore Opus 5.5 requests with Claude subscription authentication [#156062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156062) — Thanks @serg0x, @aleps001, @kiiatkin.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Claude Sonnet 5.5">

[Claude Sonnet 5.5](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic) is available through Anthropic, and the rolling `sonnet` choice now selects it. Explicit version selections, including `sonnet-5`, stay pinned, while fresh setup still defaults to Opus 5.5. Thinking defaults to high; choosing off still permits notes between tool calls.

Claude Code 2.1.277 rejects the new model ID. Use an explicit `sonnet-5` selection to keep Sonnet 5 on that version.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add Claude Sonnet 5.5 and advance the rolling `sonnet` alias, with a one-million-token context window, 128,000-token output limit, and Sonnet 5 API rates [#160847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160847)

Fast mode and Priority Tier remain unavailable. Forced tool choices become automatic. Reasoning can carry from Sonnet 5 or Claude 4.x into Sonnet 5.5, but not from Opus 5/5.5, Fable, or Mythos; other models cannot reuse Sonnet 5.5 reasoning. Eligible direct Anthropic API-key requests may fall back to Sonnet 5 after safety refusals at the same rates. This change does not add Bedrock, Vertex, Foundry, or Mantle catalog entries or establish a minimum compatible Claude Code version.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Video generation providers">

[Video generation](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/video-generation) adds Kie AI, Z.AI, and Novita for text prompts and supported image inputs. Qwen and Alibaba Wan can also animate local images. Kie is enabled by default but needs an API key, and Z.AI video uses general API billing even if you use a Coding Plan for chat. Input and output limits vary by model; Kie AI, Z.AI, and Novita do not accept reference videos.

Sora and the bundled custom OpenAI-compatible video connection have been removed following Sora's retirement. Replace saved `openai/sora-2` or `openai/sora-2-pro` selections with another configured provider. They are not changed automatically and can keep failing.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add Kie AI, Z.AI, and Novita video models and allow Kie/Runway credential-only configuration [#160080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160080)

**Bug fixes**

- Show failed video and music providers in warning logs [#158914](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158914) — Thanks @ayushdevo, @obviyus, @kybrcore.
- Animate local images with Qwen and Alibaba Wan within tool intake and model-specific limits [#160123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160123)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Remove the retired OpenAI Sora video provider [#159543](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159543) — Thanks @kybrcore.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex command results and conversations">

The managed Codex runtime moves to 0.158.0, fixing missing output from completed commands. Parent agents can continue when workers finish, and conversation forks and compaction retain their context more consistently. These fixes need the updated runtime and do not replay previously failed deliveries. [Model discovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness-reference/model-discovery) also waits longer for slow responses, unless you configured a shorter timeout.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce Codex relay processing for unchanged requests [#155987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155987)
- Release idle Codex catalog decoder memory [#156202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156202)
- Clarify deferred Codex catalog refresh warnings [#157352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157352) — Thanks @shakkernerd, @mrzeepek, @flyto168, @zl0nline, @brennan-scott, @msobrosa, @safrano9999.
- Stop full Codex history scans while catalogs are idle [#157565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157565) — Thanks @pollybot13, @siskoyu.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Codex conversations usable after compaction [b4d60fa5](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b4d60fa5edfc2713a5cb3643fa1c936fec3c3cd0) — Thanks @jjjhenriksen, @jalehman.
- Respect Codex compaction cancellation and close progress [#132343](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132343)

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex files, tools, and Ultrafast">

Local Codex can read full uploaded documents beyond their shortened previews when running without a sandbox and with read permission. Optional plugin tools already granted to the agent remain available. You can opt into [Ultrafast](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness/config-fields) with `appServer.enableUltrafast`; it is off by default and requires account and model support.

[User-home Codex connections](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness/app-server) now follow the provider configured in that Codex home, including when an existing chat resumes or forks. Check that it supports your model. To keep built-in OpenAI routing, set `model_provider = "openai"` there and restart the app-server, or use an agent-home setup with suitable credentials.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

**Bug fixes**

- Honor the native home's configured provider for user-home Codex chats, including cold resume and side questions [#156226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156226) — Thanks @galiniliev.
- Let local Codex read complete uploaded documents [#156758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156758)
- Restore Codex model-reference validation in Docker [#157175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157175) — Thanks @sandra-peach.
- Honor configured tool PATH in local Codex commands [#157290](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157290) — Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Exit normally after Codex model validation [#157323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157323) — Thanks @obviyus.
- Preserve compact argument schemas for Codex plugin tools [#157734](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157734) — Thanks @jalehman, @flyto168.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Restore local Codex admission through the earlier detached-task owner, before that Tasks surface was retired [#156624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156624)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Claude CLI conversations and tools">

Foreground Claude CLI subagents can work beyond fifteen minutes while they continue making progress; silent work still has a timeout. Manual compaction keeps the conversation connected, and permitted shell tools keep working after a plugin refresh.

Claude CLI turns managed by the local plugin now allow quiet time while Claude shortens a long conversation. The overall run deadline still applies, and sessions running on a node keep their previous timeout behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

- Avoid duplicate concurrent Claude history imports [#158232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158232)

- Preserve Claude CLI conversation context during manual compaction [#157870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157870) — Thanks @obviyus, @amarsmission.
- Keep Claude CLI shell tools working after plugin refresh [#159000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159000) — Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Anthropic sign-in and prompt caching">

[Anthropic connections](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic) handle stored subscription credentials and custom beta settings more consistently, avoiding related request failures. Prompt caching also follows the provider's context settings, and rewinding no longer changes an otherwise identical prompt unnecessarily.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Copilot accounts and saved history">

Copilot keeps credentials for different organizations separate during repair, refresh, and logout. If an agent relied on another organization's credentials, sign in for the intended organization. Newly saved conversations also preserve valid encrypted reasoning, and each agent keeps its own Code Mode controls without gaining extra tool permissions.

- Keep Copilot Code Mode controls with the executing agent [#157044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157044)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Model menus">

[Model menus](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models) show known choices while the full list loads and keep your current model easy to find. Repeated background refreshes no longer stall native agent runs. Reopen the menu to see newly discovered models; your account and role restrictions still apply.

<Accordion title="Provider errors and retries">

OpenClaw avoids repeatedly sending requests that a provider has already rejected, while temporary failures keep their normal [retry handling](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/retry). Plugin background requests can use your configured fallback models unless you explicitly chose a model. Before retrying a failed turn, check whether its tools already completed any actions.

- Keep TypeSafe input rejections from blocking valid decisions [#156746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156746) — Thanks @jalehman.
- Show provider recovery guidance when ACP returns no reply [#156879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156879)

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Streaming replies">

OpenClaw does less repeated work when receiving long replies and preparing large model requests. Buffered Anthropic replies also give other conversations time to respond.

<Accordion title="Gemini and Grok voice conversations">

Gemini 3.8 Live keeps spoken turns in history and handles requests for agent help more consistently. Grok Talk avoids duplicate saved utterances and supports longer playback. Speaking over Talk replies interrupts playback again during agent consults unless you disabled interruption; Gemini Extended Thinking may still resume speaking after an interruption.

- Restore Gemini 3.8 Live spoken turns and consults [cf982200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/cf982200a2a33624022251e49a2f44bb93118b1b) — Thanks @saariuslystoned, @iwhatsskill.
- Restore Gemini 3.8 Live and Extended Thinking replies when clients keep sending silent audio frames after speech; the bridge does not generate missing audio [#152844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152844) — Thanks @saariuslystoned.
- Restore Talk interruption during forced agent consults [#153727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153727) — Thanks @saariuslystoned, @dreamer-hit, @destinedenergy.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Gemini speech and two-voice dialogue">

You can choose Gemini 3.8 Flash or Flash-Lite to [generate speech](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/google), with delivery instructions kept separate from the words spoken. Gemini 3.1 Flash TTS Preview remains the default.

For dialogue, select a 3.8 speech model and [configure exactly two voices](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tts/field-reference), each with a distinct speaker name. Put the name and words on the same line, such as `Puck: Hello`, to choose that voice. Unrecognized labels are read aloud, and text without recognized labels uses the single-voice path.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add optional Gemini 3.8 Flash and Flash-Lite speech models with separate delivery-style metadata [#157331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157331) — Thanks @saariuslystoned.
- Generate Gemini dialogue with two explicitly configured voices and inline speaker labels [#157465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157465) — Thanks @saariuslystoned.

Set `tts.providers.google.model` to `gemini-3.8-flash-tts` or `gemini-3.8-flash-lite-tts`, and configure dialogue through `tts.providers.google.speakers`. Speaker names are case-sensitive, and a bare name without words on the same line does not switch voices. Lead-in text belongs to the first labeled speaker encountered. Older preview models reject recognized dialogue. These additions generate audio from text and do not change realtime voice conversations or automatically cast speakers.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Gemini Interactions">

You can opt into [Gemini Interactions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/google) for streamed replies, reasoning, images, and tools. Google defaults stay unchanged. This connection sends the needed history with each request without asking the API to store it, and can recover interrupted replies under the normal retry rules. Cached-content handles and video clipping offsets are not supported.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add opt-in Gemini Interactions and fix pending thinking-level edits and rollback in the Control UI [#149880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149880) — Thanks @markmcd, @romneyda, @rboy1.

**Bug fixes**

- Recover interrupted Google Interactions replies and release readers [#159198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159198)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="PDFs and unreadable images">

[PDF analysis](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/pdf) works with valid OpenAI credentials when Codex is enabled and can fall back to your active vision model when other choices are unavailable. The image viewer can skip unreadable images while keeping valid ones in the same request, preventing them from breaking a new reply.

- Use the active vision model as a final PDF fallback [#159593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159593) — Thanks @zyz619963502zyz, @obviyus, @dh-js.
- Skip unreadable viewed images before they break replies [#159668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159668) — Thanks @obviyus, @chaolawo-byte.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Usage estimates and Ultra">

Cost estimates now use the provider handling the request, and total CLI token counts are kept separate from the current conversation's context usage. Missing SuperGrok usage percentages are explained rather than shown as zero. You can also choose [Ultra](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/thinking) for planning and verification guidance across OpenClaw and Claude Code models. It uses only the reasoning the model supports; native Codex needs supported reasoning choices. Cost figures remain estimates.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Extend Ultra planning and verification across supported runtimes [#155393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155393) — Thanks @vincentkoc.

- Separate CLI token totals from current context usage [f3857241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f38572419507b23de4ffaa0f4e55175b07e2e65a) — Thanks @vincentkoc, @yuxin-qiao.
- Explain missing SuperGrok usage percentages without inventing zero usage [#155790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155790) — Thanks @saariuslystoned, @obviyus.
- Reject inherited keys in Moonshot and Tencent thinking policies [#158437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158437)
- Show the selected model's Fast Mode policy in status [#158934](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158934) — Thanks @vacinc.
- Preserve supported extra-high reasoning alongside configured context budgets on standard OpenRouter connections; already prepared models can still retain stale effort settings after a catalog refresh [#160651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160651) — Thanks @Olli0103, @obviyus, @AXEG0.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Decision model evaluations">

Agents can use a configured [Decision model](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/decision-models) to answer yes/no questions, choose between alternatives, or score supplied evidence. That evidence goes to your selected local or hosted provider, with its normal limits and charges. Results do not authorize actions, and the default-off Labs preference does not start automatic evaluations. Private or nightly setups using the old TypeSafe tool or model setting must move to the shared Decision selection.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add explicit Decision evaluation, a Labs preference without automatic work, and replacement of the old TypeSafe-specific tool and model setting [#155134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155134) — Thanks @jjjhenriksen, @jalehman, @mertbasar0.

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve runtime context after provider review [efd9b981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/efd9b9811e065d7a082ef8b8169dcdfd8fcf21ec)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="ACP and OpenClaw model settings">

ACP agents now keep their external agent's model choice separate from OpenClaw's own model calls. After updating, check `agents.defaults.model` and its credentials. This can change the provider and account used for those calls, who bills you, and where your data goes. Signing in to the external agent does not sign you in to that provider. The [model guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models) explains the settings.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Separate ACP harness models from native model calls [#153756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153756) — Thanks @saariuslystoned, @shakkernerd, @ajmtrz.
- Keep active turns working through configuration reloads [#154462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154462) — Thanks @treylawrence, @vacinc, @oldrich333.
- Keep Gateway model commands out of local state preparation [#158136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158136) — Thanks @vacinc.

**Documentation**

- Clarify that ACP steering queues a follow-up; cancel first when work needs immediate redirection [#154372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154372) — Thanks @saariuslystoned, @ubehera.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Responses API conversation continuity">

Applications using the [Responses API](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/openresponses-http-api) can continue non-Incognito conversations after a restart. Continuation lasts up to 30 days, subject to storage limits and the conversation's lifetime. If `previous_response_id` is unknown or expired, the API now returns an error instead of silently starting a new chat. Resend the full history without that ID. IDs from before this update are lost when the old Gateway exits; live Incognito chats need an authorized session key without a response ID.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid false aborted results for running async tools [#157212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157212) — Thanks @obviyus.
- Keep delayed tool results matched to their original calls across eligible Responses continuations, falling back to full history when the match is ambiguous [906dccdd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/906dccdd6aeb7969d9ccacab256d602d3d41e577) — Thanks @hartmark and Patrick Erichsen.
- Retain provider usage and error details after rejected tool calls in the built-in OpenAI Responses transport while blocking later calls [a1a0ec6c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a1a0ec6c5b553edca48d8d64f645dd0a8ae9bbc3) — Thanks @RomneyDa.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Persist response continuations and reject unresolved IDs [#159227](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159227)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Telnyx setup">

Connect Telnyx by installing its official provider with `openclaw plugins install telnyx`, then adding your API key. The [Telnyx guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/telnyx) covers setup and model selection. The plugin is installed separately, and available models depend on your account.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add official Telnyx inference provider setup [#116016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/116016) — Thanks @dynamite-bud, @jalehman, @patrick-erichsen, @dmmc12.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Ollama tool calls">

Ollama agents no longer lose free-form tool arguments because of how OpenClaw describes them. This fixes affected Tool Search calls that repeatedly failed with missing-input errors.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve free-form tool inputs in native Ollama requests [#157564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157564) — Thanks @hmache, @obviyus, @markomiric, @holny.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Fireworks default model">

New [Fireworks setups](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/fireworks) use GLM 5.3 Fast instead of the retired GLM 5.2 Fast. If you saved the old model, choose a replacement yourself. The bundled cached-input cost estimate rises from $0.21 to $0.39 per million tokens.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Replace Fireworks' retired default with GLM 5.3 Fast [#160102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160102)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Grok and DeepSeek thinking controls">

Supported newer standard Grok models get thinking controls and image-input support without waiting for each model name to be added. Grok 4.20 and variants such as Fast are excluded. DeepSeek Flash also honors thinking settings on affected OpenAI-compatible connections.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recognize thinking and images for newer standard Grok releases [#156397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156397) — Thanks @serg0x.
- Restore canonical DeepSeek Flash fallback thinking support [#157304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157304) — Thanks @sahilsatralkar, @obviyus, @anarchia-99.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Automations and Scheduling

Automations gain clearer setup, run history, and delivery status, alongside fixes for interrupted and delegated work.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Schedule settings">

Jobs scheduled for a named conversation now run in its saved project directory or managed Git worktree. If the worktree is missing or no longer matches the conversation, the job stops. Restore its binding or recreate the job from a valid bound conversation before retrying. New unbound sessions still use the agent's configured workspace, while current and isolated jobs stay detached.

The Automations editor catches invalid webhook addresses before saving and correctly saves the default timing window for hourly jobs. The [schedule guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/schedules) explains exact timing and staggered starts.

If your scripts create system-event jobs, remove `--timeout-seconds` from those commands. [Cron add and create](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/cron) now reject that previously ignored option. Agent and command jobs keep timeout support; script jobs use `--script-timeout-seconds`.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse unchanged automation list results and look up pending-message names in the selected store [#158077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158077).
- Reduce loading-related browser style work when searching expanded Automations lists [#159901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159901). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Reject malformed webhook URLs and embedded credentials in the editor before saving [#146706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146706). Thanks @pengzh1, @obviyus, and @aniruddhaadak80.
- Preserve hourly default staggering when saving and reopening timing choices [#156974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156974).
- Let agents read automation jobs and update results containing scheduler error counts [#157497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157497). Thanks @he-yufeng, @obviyus, @nicolasderito, and @xiaozishan.
- Use saved project directories for named-session schedules and retain workspace and tool restrictions across rollover, with conversation and worktree ownership checks [#159444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159444). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Identify invalid timezones separately from invalid timestamps and nonexistent daylight-saving times [#159756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159756).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Reject unsupported timeout flags on system-event creation without changing stored jobs [#159716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159716).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Announcement recipients and delivery status">

Announcement setup suggests known conversations after you select a channel and enter its Account ID, with manual entry still available. Scheduled announcements receive the channel's formatting instructions, and command or script announcements sent to an explicit destination can appear in chat history for follow-up replies.

[Webhook delivery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/delivery) shows Unknown when the request may have arrived but no response confirms it. OpenClaw does not retry these sends. A successful job is not confirmation of delivery, including when best-effort delivery lets the job finish.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Suggest account-scoped announcement targets, preserve manual entry, and clear stale topics after routing changes; failure-alert suggestions remain separate [05af7bc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/05af7bc4b306987f15b83092d4c8b366dc8f194b). Thanks @zeroaltitude.
- Reduce session-processing work for delivery previews and scheduled destination lookups while preserving routing choices and reading route changes afresh [#160216](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160216).

**Bug fixes**

- Record successful explicit-destination command and script announcements in chat context when destination lookup and session admission succeed [#156962](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156962). Thanks @obviyus and @fourestfire.
- Supply scheduled chat announcements with the delivering channel and account's formatting rules [#157877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157877). Thanks @obviyus.
- Accept disabled failure alerts through the automations tool while keeping auto-disable safety notices; restricted-schema providers retain object-only settings [#158325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158325). Thanks @obviyus and @joshpetras.
- Preserve unknown webhook delivery after timeouts, interrupted responses, and ambiguous redirects without replaying the send [#159840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159840). Thanks @joshavant.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Automation run transcripts">

Choose [View transcript](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/managing-jobs) on a run to read its conversation and load earlier messages. Isolated runs show their own conversation; custom-session jobs show the conversation shared across runs. Run history remains available after Tasks retirement, and the [sessions CLI](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/sessions) can archive or delete a stored run by its exact key. Retention limits are unchanged, and previously deleted history is not restored.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Give conversation-history reads a separate queue from cron retention scans, with an extra on-demand reader and memory and cold-start tradeoffs [#155998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155998).
- Move scheduled-job history reads off the Gateway thread while preserving filters, pagination, and current access checks [#158222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158222).
- Open and page the recorded automation transcript directly through Cron, preserving the selected session generation and checking access on every page [#158776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158776).

**Bug fixes**

- Resolve exact cron-run session keys for CLI deletion, archiving, and dry runs while retaining confirmation and identity checks [#158212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158212). Thanks @obviyus and @abacha.
- Recheck cron history grouping and current rank before overflow deletion; this change's managed-flow cancellation repair preceded the later TaskFlow removal [#159064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159064).
- Close old transcripts when switching automation panels and ignore their delayed replies while retaining same-panel selections [#159160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159160).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recovery after temporary failures">

A temporary database error during startup no longer leaves future jobs unscheduled after the problem clears. Jobs using supported local Ollama or OpenAI-compatible endpoints also [check the provider again on their next run](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/payloads) after a connection check times out. The affected run may still be skipped.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid redundant database snapshots during cron receipt checks without changing admission or deletion checks [#156801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156801).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve command output, exit status, and timeout details when cleanup fails; uncertain cleanup still reports an error and keeps backup write ownership held [c6709b4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c6709b460e637f7c0c5cf6eda4c97a17c6fc5933). Thanks @masatohoshino.
- Stop caching client-side local-provider probe timeouts for five minutes, allowing the next isolated run to probe again [#156237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156237). Thanks @obviyus and @charlie-morrison.
- Resume future scheduling after transient startup catch-up failure, including subsequent suspension and resume [#159086](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159086). Thanks @obviyus and @yetval.
- Move manual, scheduled, and startup catch-up reservations to the database worker; execution still waits for a durable reservation [#159792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159792).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Permissions for delegated work">

Scheduled jobs can launch workers with their already-approved permissions without a mistaken administrator-permission error. Configured channel owners can also manage automations again after delegated work finishes. Existing restrictions still apply, and explicitly requesting Full Access still requires administrator permission.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recognize existing one-use automation management grants after subagent completion while retaining handler validation [#157404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157404). Thanks @obviyus.
- Preserve the parent's effective execution mode for newly spawned workers without granting administrator identity or rewriting existing sessions [#158270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158270). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev and Jason Sy.
- Read scheduled-job child status and answers through the database worker while retaining successor, paused-work, queued-delivery, and cancellation handling [#160508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160508).
- Omit tool discovery and delegation instructions from Codex runs with an explicitly empty tool allowlist, including scheduled jobs; this corrects the prompt without adding permission enforcement [#160840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160840).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Heartbeat alerts and restarts">

When a completed [heartbeat](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/heartbeat) decides no notification is needed, it also suppresses tool-failure warnings and attachments. Later runner failures still produce alerts. Restarting no longer hangs on a queued heartbeat job; that waiting job is marked skipped during shutdown.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Settle queued heartbeat jobs as skipped during shutdown to avoid a circular restart wait [24babfe](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/24babfe94446197e4d9dd35dac8a14556943d7f5). Credit Peter Steinberger.
- Honor completed quiet heartbeat decisions and suppress their exact unsent warning records; already queued or ambiguous sends keep their recovery handling [#156174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156174). Thanks @pash-openai.
- Correct Doctor's create and update summaries for disabled monitors without changing their stored enabled state [#159711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159711).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Workboard completion triggers">

Enabled automations attached to a [Workboard](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/workboard) can now run when linked workers finish, fixing cases where they had to wait for the next scheduled run. Disabled jobs stay off, and the normal schedule remains a fallback. Older hosts without this trigger support report that it is unavailable.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Let the active Workboard service request completion-triggered runs while retaining disabled-job, cron-origin, and stopped-service exclusions [#156130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156130). Thanks @joshavant.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Gmail watch renewal">

[Gmail watches](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/gmail) renew once after an overdue wake and avoid overlapping renewal attempts. Stopping or replacing a watcher also stops its unfinished renewal. The renewal schedule and mail forwarding behavior are unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Coordinate Gmail watch renewal with scheduling, replacement, and shutdown in the Gateway and standalone Gmail service [#158913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158913).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Scheduled-session cleanup">

Cleanup of old scheduled sessions does more work in the background and uses less temporary memory, reducing interruptions to other activity. Retention rules and transcript archiving are unchanged, and cleanup can still take longer overall in custom stores. The [scheduling guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/architecture#timed-work-and-shutdown) explains late starts, startup catch-up, and shutdown.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Discover expired cron-run sessions in a worker while retaining shared-store ownership and deletion safeguards [#155414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155414).
- Reduce temporary allocation during cleanup and previews without making previews persistent or changing retention policy [#160510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160510).

**Documentation**

- Explain coalesced late timer wakes, Cron's own execution and catch-up policy, and shutdown waiting for registered work [#160477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160477).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Browser and Computer Use

The chat Browser panel shows tabs related to your conversation, and attached or cloud-session desktops let you hand control back to the agent after a manual step.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Lightpanda browser profiles">

Experimental [Lightpanda profiles](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/browser/lightweight) let your agent read pages and fill forms. Lightpanda needs separate setup with a protected connection and supports one temporary page at a time. After a disconnect, reopen the page and take a new snapshot. Chromium remains the default for screenshots, PDFs, file transfers, saved sign-ins, multiple tabs, and unsupported sites.

Check the guide's licensing requirements before deploying or redistributing Lightpanda. Version 0.4.1 uses AGPL-3.0-or-later; OpenClaw's adapter remains MIT-licensed.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add explicitly selected, externally started Lightpanda profiles with capability checks and connection-owned pages. [b7eb012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b7eb012f643b24b561fe67d974c1b643788114b5) — @vincentkoc
- Show supported browser engines and their session and screenshot capabilities in status, without implying an engine is installed or running. [0a5178c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0a5178c483b5aecfc4a27ecffccf69323c2d52fd) — @vincentkoc
- Add an optional Chromium headless-shell benchmark selection with distinct result labels; this does not change the production browser or establish resource savings. [f5cf1b3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f5cf1b31e1fc988b1dc225cb6152f2d683ee9e2e) — @vincentkoc

**Documentation**

- Add pinned native Linux/macOS and Linux-container setup examples, plus an opt-in text-and-form benchmark; Windows guidance uses containers or WSL. [645f1bc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/645f1bc4ef49f9056b54d39b9c4ab7219776c1b3) — @vincentkoc
- Document engine licensing, artifact provenance, and redistribution obligations in the September 21 audit, without treating checksums as permission to redistribute. [322e358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/322e358bb0ca00ce92ebe2ddc8ce9d23f652c8f1) — @vincentkoc

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browser tabs in chat">

The chat [Browser panel](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/browser/profiles) now shows tabs related to that conversation. The tagged [Android](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/android#agent-browser-in-chat) source lets you interact with the agent's tab inside chat and return to your draft (see [Native Apps](#native-apps) for release availability). This requires administrator access and an updated Gateway with its bundled Control UI enabled. Closing the Android card leaves the tab open. New tabs opened through the Browser panel close when you reset or delete the conversation; older tabs remain in the Browser dock.

[Session browser dashboards](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/dashboards) also let people with conversation write access interact without browser administrator access. They start without your saved sign-ins and require Playwright with local managed Chromium. Conversation access limits still apply, and read-only, sandbox-required, or locked-model sessions are excluded. Stopping the dashboard, resetting the conversation, or restarting OpenClaw loses its page state.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Limit chat Browser tab listings to session-owned and conversation-referenced tabs, with session cleanup for newly panel-opened tabs and the full Browser dock still available. [#159891](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159891)
- Add Android in-chat remote browser control, touch and keyboard input, collapse, and reopening under existing administrator access. [#159767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159767) — @iwhatsskill, @ivankuznetsov
- Let eligible session writers interact with isolated managed-Chromium dashboards, separate from administrator browsing; remote, attached, personal, node, and Lightpanda profiles are unsupported, and dashboard records remain in memory with a 64-record limit. [#156371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156371) — @vincentkoc

**Bug fixes**

- Open the browser card's selected page after the panel finishes delayed rendering. [#153253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153253) — @vyctorbrzezowski
- Give browser streaming one startup window of up to 30 seconds before cancelling a stalled attempt and falling back to a screenshot, followed by the existing retry. [#156323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156323) — @vincentkoc
- Keep ordinary Chrome tabs usable when Chrome Web Store or another recognized protected page refuses control; the protected page stays excluded and no extension update is required. [#156890](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156890) — @holny, @obviyus, @liyoulu
- Refit visible shared browser views after remote resizing and discard late native-browser errors from a previous tab or session. [#157087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157087)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Shared desktop control">

After you sign in or finish a manual step on a [conversation-attached or cloud-session desktop](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/desktop), you can ask the agent to continue without separately releasing control in the viewer. It uses its existing permission, takes a fresh screenshot, and does not replay interrupted input. You can take control back at any time. This handoff is limited to attached and cloud-session desktops.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add explicit agent takeover after manual control, invalidate old observations, preserve human reclaim, and show a neutral reconnect message after a clean viewer disconnect. [#159923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159923)
- Update macOS desktop automation to Peekaboo 4.6.0 for capture and input fixes; contended actions time out if they cannot start within 15 seconds, while already executing actions keep their locks. [#158022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158022)

**Bug fixes**

- Treat blank optional computer selectors as omitted while retaining errors for invalid or unknown nonblank selections. [#155072](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155072) — @ceckert, @obviyus
- When tool-loop detection is enabled, recognize repeated reads of an unchanged window despite fresh element references and apply the existing no-progress stop. [#160019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160019) — @obviyus, @grape404

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Linux desktop audio">

[Managed Linux desktop views](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-browser-ui-desktop) now have an Unmute control for listening to application sound, including with view-only access. Audio starts muted, stops when you hide or disconnect the view, and stays muted after reconnecting. It carries no microphone audio, uses about 1.5 Mbit/s, and may not stay in sync with the picture.

If sound is unavailable, use the Audio unavailable control for setup help. Install `pulseaudio` and `pulseaudio-utils`, then restart the managed desktop. Successful setup replaces its existing PulseAudio route with a private output; failed setup leaves the old route in place without capturing it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add optional audio availability information to desktop observations while preserving screen-only clients. [#157718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157718) — @vincentkoc
- Capture a private managed-Linux application output and recover audio independently of healthy desktop applications; exhausted recovery leaves audio disabled with restart guidance. [#157720](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157720) — @vincentkoc
- Report the current audio source after recovery, with setup guidance and sanitized failure information. [#157723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157723) — @vincentkoc
- Add explicit Unmute and Mute controls to embedded and docked desktop panels, with stop-on-hide and muted reconnect behavior. [#157724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157724) — @vincentkoc
- Add the same audio controls to the standalone Desktop view and keep its toolbar usable on narrow screens. [#157726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157726) — @vincentkoc
- Move optional audio setup guidance into the Audio unavailable control while retaining playback-error alerts. [#159487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159487)

**Security and trust**

- Require screen authentication and explicit start before audio capture, using expiring one-use bearer tickets, and end audio with its screen observation. [#157722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157722) — @vincentkoc

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex Computer Use on Mac">

[Codex Computer Use](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-computer-use) works with the replacement plugin and newer Desktop app layouts on Mac. Restart the Gateway after updating OpenClaw to load the fix. macOS permissions and your existing tool restrictions still apply; custom legacy policies may need updating.

If you disabled the old Computer Use plugin, automatic replacement now respects that choice. An owner or authorized administrator can run `/codex computer-use install` explicitly. To restore automatic setup, re-enable the old plugin setting as well.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prepare the replacement macOS Computer Use runtime and refresh changed desktop assets and sibling plugins while respecting custom identities and native policy. [#156556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156556) — @bdjben, @shakkernerd
- Recognize newer signed Desktop executable layouts and create writable isolated launch configuration without changing the signed app or its permissions. [#158480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158480) — @jalehman
- Honor an explicit native disable during automatic setup, preserve explicit owner/admin installation, and fix symlinked cache discovery and stale launchers. [#160133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160133) — @bdjben

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browser and desktop support on Bun">

Bun installations no longer need a separate Node installation for the packaged Chrome connector or managed-desktop control helper. Custom browser commands keep their existing behavior, and Windows extension connections still need `node.exe`.

[Terminals on Bun](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/bun-compatibility#known-limitations) have separate requirements. Node-free terminals on macOS and Linux need a compatible Bun build; stock Bun 1.4.2 still needs Node. This update does not change the apps' Bun versions. Some Bun builds can also take about 30 seconds to close a desktop command that launched an app.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Launch packaged Chrome MCP with OpenClaw's current runtime on the next browser session, preserving custom commands. [#159422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159422)
- Avoid the packaged Chrome server's npm startup update probe while retaining custom servers' existing checks. [#160226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160226)
- Run managed desktop control with the Gateway's executable, removing that helper's separate Node requirement. [#159462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159462)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Enable native terminals on compatible macOS/Linux Bun builds, retaining the Node helper for stock Bun and node-pty for Windows and Node runtimes. [#159447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159447)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Desktop cleanup and shutdown">

Desktops prepared for a cancelled or rejected action are now released when the run ends, with cleanup failures reported. Browser activity also avoids pauses caused by saving tab records to a busy database. [Node shutdown](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/node-host) waits for cleanup to finish, so a stuck plugin can still block shutdown or an automatic node update indefinitely.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Await node and desktop-worker cleanup before reporting shutdown complete, and require reconnecting after failed plugin disconnect cleanup. [#156995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156995)
- Retain responsibility for releasing an attached desktop when setup is cancelled, without sending the cancelled input. [#157997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157997) — @vincentkoc
- Release every attached-desktop preparation at run end after rejected actions, including preparations that were never selected for use. [#158546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158546) — @vincentkoc
- Move an in-root Browser symlink to Trash without moving its outside or missing destination, while continuing to refuse escaped parent directories. [#158640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158640)
- Move Browser tab database work off the main thread while preserving tab ownership, retention, and completion of accepted work during shutdown. [#159599](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159599)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Search accounts and browser connections">

Automatic [web search](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/web) now uses an available xAI sign-in before a Tavily key, falling back to Tavily if Grok fails. A provider you selected explicitly keeps its existing behavior. Browser connection fixes also preserve supplied login credentials and correctly detect Windows browsers installed in paths with spaces.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore OAuth-aware Grok-before-Tavily automatic search selection while preserving explicit provider pins and their no-fallback behavior. [#156048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156048) — @holny, @obviyus, @darkjuanfra
- Preserve CDP authorization supplied as Headers objects or header tuples, including explicit-header precedence over URL credentials. [#159430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159430)
- Detect Windows browser versions in paths with spaces and keep failed probes quiet, retaining the existing conditional directory fallback. [#160432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160432) — @irish-joseph, @obviyus, @avirtudazo-officeconnect, @ayushdevo

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Plugins and Integrations

Plugin pages now explain what an integration does and how to set it up, alongside fixes for plugin updates, cloud sessions, and connected coding agents.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Cloud sessions and recovery">

Stopping one cloud session no longer blocks unrelated sessions from starting. Interrupted downloads can resume, and compatible [warm images](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/warm-images) skip unnecessary snapshot preparation. An interrupted Stop can also [resume saving your workspace](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) after a restart or update, provided the original machine is still available.

Messages sent during a cloud worker runtime update wait for it to finish before starting. Slow setup downloads get longer windows based on archive size and can continue while data is arriving, but initial pairing credentials still expire after ten minutes, so a slow first setup can still fail at pairing.

A lost machine leaves only the last saved workspace, and forced destruction discards unsaved edits. Failed cleanup can leave a machine running and charging you. If automatic cleanup gives up, inspect and stop the Crabbox lease, then retry Stop in the conversation.

All Crabbox providers now require **0.67.0 or newer**. If your host cannot download updates, install a supported executable before updating OpenClaw or inspection and cleanup may fail. Azure, Daytona, and GCP operators should check the [Crabbox migration notes](https://github.com/openclaw/crabbox/releases/tag/v0.67.0).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Defer replacement snapshots for compatible changed-commit warm starts; disabling reserves also disables automatic image refresh [#156380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156380)
- Reduce remote snapshot-preparation commands and allow slower configured CLI probes [#156767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156767)
- Prepare first-dispatch runtime and worker archives concurrently [#159520](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159520)

- Avoid full file application when both remote and local workspaces remain unchanged [#160231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160231)

**Bug fixes**

- Keep active worker history and Stop requests tied to their original session store [#156452](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156452)

<Accordion title="Application previews on attached workers">

You can open interactive [application previews](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/portals) on a conversation's dedicated attached secondary worker when your write and tool permissions allow it. Sessions that require a sandbox or lock model selection are excluded. Anyone with the preview link can use it while it is open, even if the person who created it loses access. Close the preview to withdraw the link.

Foreground apps on supported Linux workers can also use [host-held model API keys](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/secrets/secret-store-and-egress#model-credentials-for-crabbox-commands) without copying the real key to the worker. This needs a supported API-key connection and a proxy-compatible app; OAuth and detached apps are unsupported. If cleanup fails, check the remote app before reusing the worker. Revoking its key access does not stop the app.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Run prepared foreground Crabbox applications with command-scoped host credentials; source and dependencies must already be present, and the grant does not contain network access or restrict model operations [#156207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156207)
- Open, list, and close previews on the conversation's dedicated attached worker; reconnect or update an unavailable node before retrying [#156373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156373) — @vincentkoc

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Plugin details and setup">

Plugin pages show capabilities, setup guides, and the inputs their tools expect, so you can understand an integration before using it. Enabled image, video, and music providers also appear under Media. [Customize UI](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/feature-plugins) is now in Advanced settings, with `/settings/plugins?tab=advanced` available to restore the built-in interface. A capability listing does not mean your account is connected or access is granted.

<Accordion title="Plugin installation and updates">

You can install a package without changing whether it is enabled by using `openclaw plugins install <package> --no-enable`. Bun-only installations can manage npm plugins without separately installing Node or npm. When an install fails, the interface shows whether to retry or check its status first.

[Plugin updates](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/plugins/uninstall-and-update) can apply supported settings repairs before activation, but check migrated listener settings afterward. Data migrations still need `openclaw doctor --fix`, and disabled plugins keep their pending changes. If an older updater rejects legacy settings, update OpenClaw itself first. An unconfirmed rollback leaves the matching package in place and reports the failure.

<Accordion title="Plugin reloads and removal">

[Plugin reloads](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/plugins/uninstall-and-update) can wait for work already in progress. Use `openclaw plugins reload <ids...> --wait` outside a conversation currently using that plugin to wait beyond the usual 60 seconds. Cancelling after the replacement takes effect does not undo it. Reload does not rebuild code, and some compiled changes still require a restart; browser-only changes can use **Reload plugin UI**.

After a forced stop during startup or plugin reload, OpenClaw can avoid an abandoned operation’s five-minute wait once it confirms the old process has exited on the same machine. Older operations without that process information and package installs still wait for their existing expiry.

Removal stops if settings reference the plugin again. Remove those references before retrying. Interrupted removal can leave the plugin disabled with cleanup still pending, and files already deleted are not restored.

- Move post-activation cleanup index reads off the caller thread [#159254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159254)

<Accordion title="Plugin files and temporary storage">

OpenClaw keeps loaded native plugin files until it exits, then can reclaim retired copies at a later startup. Cleanup of older temporary files can still disrupt an older running process if OpenClaw cannot identify it. Plugin authors must replace shared temporary roots such as `/tmp` with a private, user-owned directory that others cannot write to, using the existing secure-root helper.

[LanceDB statistics](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/memory-lancedb) now limit database reads to 60 seconds and return zero when the memory table is missing. Plugin startup can take additional time.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Remove capture payloads before ownership metadata and reclaim eligible aged scratch after interrupted cleanup [#157727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157727) — @yyds-xxxx
- Enforce existing Team Reports and Google Chat file limits while files are being read [#158181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158181)
- Retain loaded native addons through process exit and bound nonmutating LanceDB statistics reads [#158950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158950) — @blackeyes-boy

**Limits and compatibility**

- Require private supplied temporary roots for backup, installation, and SDK workspace callers [#159327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159327)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="MCP tool discovery">

[MCP servers](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/mcp) get more time to list their tools, helping slower servers make them available in new chats and scheduled runs. Repeated startup failures show when to retry instead of making every run wait again. After signing in, tools may still take up to ten minutes to reappear unless you explicitly reload MCP in the process using it.

<Accordion title="ACP sessions and recovery">

Connected ACP coding agents accept prompts again after a settings-triggered restart, and [Stop](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/acp-agents/runbook) waits for work and cleanup to finish. Some cancellation and reset paths can still affect a replacement session. Codex and Claude can recover from missing adapter files; restart OpenClaw after updating to refresh their launchers. Recovery may need internet access to download an uncached adapter.

[ACPX session storage](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/acp-agents/quickstart) moves to a writable location. Existing sessions move automatically only when that destination is empty. Check your sessions after updating; if they disappear from view, their old files may still be present, and the existing `stateDir` setting lets you keep using that location.

<Accordion title="OpenCode sessions">

You can browse [OpenCode v2 sessions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/reference/opencode), read their transcripts, and track activity again, with v1 still supported. Activity tracking also works for initially empty sessions. If listing takes too long, OpenClaw reports an error instead of showing a misleading empty list.

<Accordion title="Meeting captions and transcripts">

Meeting captions and saved transcripts can keep speaker labels and information about where each caption came from. Those labels help you follow the conversation but do not verify a speaker's identity. Transcript saving and lookup do less repeated work, and failed audio startup cleans up any output process it already started. The experimental Google Meet interface does not yet provide native chat, reactions, or hand raising.

<Accordion title="Team Reports collection">

[Team Reports](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/team-reports) keeps existing daily, weekly, and monthly reports when GitHub or Discord collection fails. A first failed collection leaves the affected period unpublished. Check status warnings and regenerate the affected day once access returns. Report generation moves more work into the background. After changing Discord channels or collection filters, manually regenerate affected days so those changes are included.

<Accordion title="Plugin development tools">

Plugin authors can reuse [workspace-context helpers](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-entrypoints) and utilities for handling models and replies. Plugins using the new exports must declare a compatible OpenClaw version through `openclaw.compat.pluginApi`. Visitor-management tools also return usable lists and invitation links in Code Mode. Revoke existing visitor grants before changing their configured account, application, or policy.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Let plugins opt into Gateway-owned listeners that preserve existing webhook callback ports [11a11ef](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/11a11ef164d7caaf01e6f089324a8482c28168b3)
- Publish the existing node cancellation message contract with invocation and node identifiers [#115390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/115390) — @giodl73-repo, @patrick-erichsen
- Reduce primitive iterator-read overhead during streamed replies [#158346](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158346)
- Expose shared workspace-context and root-instruction preparation to harness developers [#158389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158389) — @sjf-oa, @sjf
- Reuse prototype checks within each streamed message graph [#158501](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158501)
- Reuse prepared HTTP route paths while preserving request validation and routing precedence [#158591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158591)
- Reduce redundant processing of nested plugin stream results [#158799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158799)
- Expose five model-normalization, stream-transform, prefill, and assistant-message helpers [#160038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160038)

**Bug fixes**

- Reject explicitly blank native-hook relay timeouts while preserving the omitted-value default [f62a5c8](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f62a5c8e9e064e6daeb03ce4877c51ed30dd124f) — @hugenshen
- Stop the agent-session SDK from treating an empty environment variable's name as a credential [#155558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155558) — @alix-007, @obviyus, @aniruddhaadak80
- Contain asynchronous reply-observer failures without holding up delivery receipts [#155691](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155691) — @sunnyshu0925, @obviyus
- Return normal aborted results for in-flight plugin completion cancellation [#156565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156565)
- Preserve stable permission-denial codes for direct and isolated completions [#156604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156604) — @shakkernerd
- Restore URL validation and annotation-only email and UUID handling [#156855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156855)
- Keep plugin-provided environments through repeated command preparation [#158378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158378)
- Bundle the standalone SDK's private session-URL dependency [#159279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159279)
- Let affected channel and voice paths operate without optional async diagnostics on older hosts; Telegram's separate 2026.9.6 Doctor dependency was addressed by the later repair under Messaging / Webhook setup on the Gateway port [#159280](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159280)
- Permit replacement login after cancellation, settle throwing channel cleanup, release failed media readers, retain remote-skill labels, and redact cyclic migration reports [#159299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159299)
- Connect selected agent tools to services in their admitting Gateway [#159410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159410)
- Return structured visitor lists, invitation links, and revoke outcomes to Code Mode [#159453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159453)
- Preserve the standard completion-failure error when an isolated adapter rejects without a value [#159600](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159600)
- Retain valid falsy targets in generic webhook matching, detect multiple matches, and omit empty-key anomaly logs; null or undefined targets in auth-or-reject wrappers retain an unresolved response limitation [#160883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160883)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Paired nodes and remote Codex">

Already-paired nodes can reconnect with `--pair-if-needed` after their setup code expires, provided their saved credentials still match the selected Gateway. New pairing still needs a fresh code. If you use a saved certificate fingerprint, verify the selected fingerprint before reconnecting because this recovery path does not compare it. [Remote Codex connections](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness/app-server) can also retry brief connection failures before sending work; model and tool requests already sent are not replayed.

Chat now shows download and installation progress when preparing a paired session host, and setup failures point to the needed repair. Older 2026.9.6 hosts can keep running turns with their supported tools; update OpenClaw on the node and restart it to use newer tools such as presence.

Compatible [paired hosts](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/session-hosting) can keep up to two idle workers loaded for two minutes to reduce startup work for follow-up messages. Those workers continue to use memory while idle. Stop or reclaim the placement to release them sooner; Chat Stop without an active turn does not release them.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Await prepared node workspace storage and release late acquisitions after cancellation; async SDK callers need a supporting host [2e9a2ec](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2e9a2eceb11bd937a64dc01fb7894ff23eb80ef7)
- Reuse idle workers when the Gateway, node, and bundle support it, retaining up to two workers for two minutes and making their capacity reclaimable [968a08c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/968a08c6d0eee2835f87b034d7dde37660a591cc)
- Show runtime transfer and installation progress for interactive paired-host setup; use Gateway logs for background reconciliation [#160110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160110)

**Bug fixes**

- Reuse saved scoped node credentials after fallback code expiry without sending the expired bootstrap token [#159360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159360) — @kevinlin-openai
- Retry transient unopened remote Codex connections up to three times during harness replacement [#159466](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159466) — @kimiyu-186
- Limit launches to each node’s supported tools, with a 2026.9.6 fallback; update and restart older nodes for newer tools [#160147](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160147)
- Explain session-host setup failures while retaining other approved node functions and retrying pending pairing; permission repairs remain manual, and already-paused clients may need one restart [#160188](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160188)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Session Share and collaboration">

Chat can start while [Session Share](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/session-share) waits for a slow paired source, with available sessions appearing when the list arrives. Resetting or deleting a session no longer shuts down ClickClack discussions elsewhere, and Workboard action menus size themselves to their contents.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Share matching Session Share catalog requests and publish later results; targeted reads and pagination keep complete responses [#156400](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156400)

**Bug fixes**

- Keep ClickClack discussions running after session cleanup and repair bundled setup loading [#157098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157098) — @masatohoshino
- Size Workboard card action rows and menus to their contents [#157913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157913) — @patrick-erichsen

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="GitHub dates and connections">

GitHub badges show when an item was merged or closed in your browser's local time, and closed draft pull requests are correctly marked **Closed**. Personal GitHub connections also skip unnecessary background checks while their tokens are fresh.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Match reader badge dates to merge or closure events, with creation-date fallback [#158081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158081) — @vincentkoc

**Improvements**

- Skip periodic refresh coordination for fresh personal GitHub tokens [#158525](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158525)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="1Password approvals">

[1Password requests](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/onepassword) save approvals in the background. Secret access still waits for a successfully saved approval, with the existing expiry and single-use rules.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Move pending approval storage into a worker while preserving single-use consumption and failed-write denial [#156000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156000)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="QA Lab keyboard navigation">

QA Lab skips hidden sidebar controls when you navigate with the keyboard. Dashboard refreshes keep your selected tab, keyboard focus, and horizontal scroll position.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Make collapsed and Evidence Archive sidebars inactive for keyboard navigation [#152189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152189) — @vincentkoc
- Preserve tab focus and scroll through polling refreshes and width changes [#155284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155284) — @vincentkoc

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Plugin loading">

Hermes migration, LLM Task, and TypeSafe now wait until first use to load their execution code, reducing work when those features are available but unused. Large plugin tool sets also avoid repeated setup work. When prepared plugins cover a turn’s needs, OpenClaw reuses them instead of copying and registering them again.

- Keep LLM Task metadata available while deferring execution code [#157233](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157233) — @vincentkoc

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Beam sidebar refreshes">

The [Beam sidebar](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/beam) does less work when refreshing its session list. Local changes trigger a refresh, while changes from other processes are checked periodically. Ongoing uploads can still delay a fresh listing.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="MXC sandbox cleanup">

When MXC cannot prepare a command, it removes the temporary sandbox directory instead of leaving it behind. The original error remains available to help diagnose the failure.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Voice Call compatibility">

[Voice Call](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/voice-call/realtime-and-streaming) loads again on OpenClaw 2026.9.6. Optional identity and workspace context remains off unless you enable it on that version.

</details>
</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Security and Privacy

You have more control over who can use OpenClaw, which models they can use, and whether they can upload files.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Incognito conversations">
