[Incognito conversations](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session) now expire 24 hours after creation or when the Gateway restarts, whichever comes first. Activity does not extend the deadline. Expiry stops active work and discards the conversation without an archive, so save anything you need beforehand. Reloading or closing the tab also discards unsent drafts and attachments.

Incognito skips automatic memory and learning, and keeps conversation content out of saved browser copies and supported diagnostic logs and plugin observers. Tabs left open from the older UI must reload after updating to apply this browser protection, so save anything you need first. Older logs remain. Tools can still write files, and model providers can still retain messages.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Exclude Incognito from persistent browser snapshots and permanently remove older private snapshots and metadata without backup. Reload already-open older tabs; an older-UI rollback may reset disposable ordinary browser cache. [#154656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154656) — @vincentkoc
- Skip automatic session-memory capture, Skill Workshop learning, and LanceDB recall embeddings for Incognito conversations. [#155594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155594) — @vincentkoc
- Suppress private content in cache traces, Anthropic payload logs, raw model-stream logs, and WebSocket previews while retaining operational metadata and live delivery. [#155595](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155595) — @vincentkoc
- Withhold Incognito LLM observations from plugins and send content-free completion callbacks for cleanup; policy, provider, approval, and explicit tool hooks remain active. [#156417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156417) — @vincentkoc
- Remove private content from subsequent delegated Task and TaskFlow record writes before those runtimes were retired; older stored records were not scrubbed. [#156446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156446) — @vincentkoc
- Suppress CLI and Gateway agent reply content in ordinary diagnostics, alongside prospective Task-record filtering implemented before Task retirement. Earlier recorded content remains. [#157543](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157543)

**Improvements**

- Add the fixed 24-hour Incognito lifetime, stopping active work and deleting history without an archive. Failed cleanup retries while new work remains blocked, so physical deletion can lag expiry. [#155596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155596) — @vincentkoc

**Bug fixes**

- Discard unsent Incognito drafts on reload while preserving same-page Settings/Back navigation and separate recovery for already-submitted tab-only outbox messages. [#156398](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156398)
- Offer a fresh Incognito conversation after expiry, retain failed unsent text for copying or discarding, and keep private attachments out of persistent browser storage. [#156544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156544)
- Drain exact-session terminals before Incognito reset deletes state; failed drainage keeps the session for retry. Controller, worker, and worktree teardown remain separate. [#159207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159207)

**Documentation**

- Explain the 24-hour-or-restart limit in the English Incognito tooltip and notice. [#156478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156478)
- Align the Control UI guide with the creation-based lifetime and existing privacy limits. [#156485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156485)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Model limits and Visitor access">

Administrators can [choose which models each role can use](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/operator-scopes#named-operator-roles), including in supported native Codex runs. Removing a model stops affected requests. Visitor Access now requires an explicit `modelPolicy`; `{}` uses the source agent's models. Restricted Codex runs are refused if their connection cannot enforce the policy.

Invalid Codex network-proxy settings can now prevent startup. Run `openclaw doctor --fix` for blank `networkProxy.profileName` or `remoteWorkspaceRoot` values; other invalid fields need manual correction.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add optional named-role model limits across selection, retries, child work, and live policy changes. Staff roles without a policy retain existing access; this is not a spending budget. [#156346](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156346) — @shakkernerd

**Security and trust**

- Extend role limits to actual native Codex inference and require explicit Visitor model policies. [17d2faf6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/17d2faf6b710196f699b351b2fdbb09ff11932e2) — @shakkernerd
- Filter disallowed structured model and provider metadata from authorized historical session responses without rewriting conversation text, tool payloads, or stored data. [#157821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157821) — @vincentkoc

**Bug fixes**

- Reject invalid enabled-proxy settings and provide Doctor repairs for the two supported blank fields while preserving the domain map. [#156906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156906) — @kevinlin-openai
- Correct false below-GPT-5 audit warnings for matching newer GPT generations, including GPT-6. Older-model and separate small-model warnings remain; this classification does not establish model safety. [#140012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/140012) — @holny, @IWhatsskill, @boycez, @Niriakot, @PollyBot13

**Limits and compatibility**

Restricted native execution excludes unowned attachments, local native models, AWS-signed requests, system proxies, custom native certificates, and incompatible proxy settings. Custom providers need explicit `base_url` and `query_params`; selecting beyond eight projected routes requires a fresh managed connection. Guardian and configured memory processing retain their service authority. Without qualified hooks, unrestricted staff work with ambiguous mixed-input attribution can continue under a valid receiver; this is not universal original-sender revocation. Historical metadata filtering does not cover every live event or model name in message text.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="File and image upload controls">

Operators can [turn off client file and image uploads](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-gateway#disable-file-and-image-uploads) with `gateway.uploads.enabled=false` while text chat keeps working, without a restart. Uploads are enabled by default. Turning them off preserves draft attachments and lets already accepted uploads finish; downloads, channel media, and agent-created files follow their own settings.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add live upload controls across documented chat, companion, terminal, avatar, skill, Workboard, node/tool, and OpenAI-compatible API inputs, with final-write checks and accepted-request retry handling. [#158567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158567)

**Limits and compatibility**

The switch does not revoke shell, filesystem, administration, or arbitrary plugin capabilities. Third-party tools that persist client bytes must carry the provided final-write callback themselves.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Tool approvals and permissions">

Approval handling now preserves the original permissions through waits and recovery. [Approval diagnostics](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/approvals) also explain why terminal tools are unavailable. If you used **Disable All**, repeat it and **Save** to include previously omitted GitHub and transcript tools.

Native Codex apps keep their configured approval settings, and permitted reads can ask for consent. Check [explicit tool exceptions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-native-plugins#approval-decision-order) separately. Do not rely on the interactive destructive-action setting alone to disable explicitly enabled native tools.

Shell approvals now reject combined options that read commands from standard input instead of the reviewed script. If a command such as `bash -se job.sh` is refused, invoke the script directly when that is the code you intend to run.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve native Codex app approval modes and reviewers across new, resumed, and side-question threads. This configuration repair does not establish complete destructive-denial enforcement for saved-enabled native tools. [1a7ba53b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1a7ba53b45ed87ba93b5ee7bf986861b4068ddfe) — @stevenlee-oai
- Restore Allow once/Deny consent for recognized native app reads and bound-app side questions; exact-tool eligibility remains a separate, unresolved enforcement limit. [#152085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152085) — @stevenlee-oai
- Include GitHub identity, GitHub publishing, and transcript tools in Disable All without changing saved permission-group membership. [#156865](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156865)
- Retain captured permissions and cancellation checks when rebuilding tool hooks, including authorized Crabbox portal presentation. [#159532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159532)
- Keep original owner access after successful or failed child follow-ups in the same conversation, while retaining revocation, reassignment, and replacement-turn checks. [#160018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160018) — @vacinc

**Security and trust**

- Recheck approval authority through database waits and recover definite storage refusals without extending deadlines or blindly replaying uncertain writes. Disconnection alone does not revoke accepted work. [#150237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150237)
- Bind worker approvals to the original live claim and cancel pending approvals when that claim is invalidated. [#155898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155898)
- Preserve original request permissions when Gateway handlers validate and dispatch requests. [#156521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156521)
- Reject combined shell stdin flags during script-file approval binding, while preserving ordinary arguments after the script filename. This is a scoped parser repair, not a general ban on stdin execution. [#160698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160698) — @eleqtrizit

**Improvements**

- Explain missing terminal tools in Agent Tools and `exec-policy show`, with local `--agent` inspection and read-only saved-session `--session` previews. A preview does not prove execution access; remote previews still show local approval facts. [#157014](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157014) — @joshavant, @pegorim

**Documentation**

- Explain native app admission, tool exceptions, approval modes, reviewers, and stricter scheduled-run authority. [#155089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155089) — @stevenlee-oai
- Correct exec-denial directions and Gateway-versus-node inspection commands. Missing TUI approval cards should not be confused with the manual `/approve` path for an authorized pending request. [#159266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159266)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browser connections and access errors">

Closing a browser tab no longer causes false server errors. If access expires while a request is waiting, OpenClaw returns an access error or closes a response already in progress. Operators investigating connection failures on a shared network can use the [connection rate-limit guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security/rate-limiting).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Handle disconnected and revoked HTTP requests without false unhandled errors, while retaining logs for genuine exceptions. [#157501](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157501)
- Close partially registered connections after initial handshake failure and discard queued requests. [#158504](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158504)
- Remove abandoned response headers so authorization changes produce correctly framed, uncached denials while preserving security and CORS headers. [#158669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158669)
- Return complete HTTP rejection responses, including oversized-upload errors and queued HEAD rejections, on affected newer Bun builds instead of premature connection resets. Node and older Bun behavior and the selected runtime version are unchanged. [#160329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160329)

**Documentation**

- Explain the existing 32 unauthenticated sockets per resolved IP and configurable override, plus bounded queue scheduling slices. Raising the socket budget admits more unauthenticated connections and requires restart; the 128-socket example is not a new default. [#158535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158535)

That documentation describes the earlier limit. The final default in this release is 128 pending unauthenticated sockets per resolved IP. To restore 32, set `OPENCLAW_MAX_PREAUTH_CONNECTIONS_PER_IP=32` and restart the Gateway.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Session resets and deletion">

[Resets that include sessions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/reset) now remove SQLite conversation history and its archives. Check the dry-run first, because the selected scope also removes configuration and OAuth credentials. Legacy JSON/JSONL import files remain. An incomplete reset reports failure without restoring anything it already deleted.

Failed remote agent deletion or session cleanup now leaves the computer running the command untouched. Restore the remote connection or run the command on the Gateway host before retrying.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Allow intentional configuration shrinkage during authenticated agent deletion without weakening unrelated write protections. [#156853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156853) — @fuller-stack-dev
- Finish database resource cleanup before reporting agent deletion complete, retaining failed closure for retry. [#157631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157631)
- Continue valid bulk session actions after an individual key lookup fails; transport and credential failures remain command-wide. [#159170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159170)
- Prevent failed remote `agents delete` and `sessions cleanup --enforce` from falling back to local mutations. Explicit local-store and dry-run operations retain their own scope. [#159223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159223)
- Return a failing exit status for incomplete resets and omit the premature onboarding hint. [#159250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159250)
- Remove canonical SQLite session history during session-inclusive resets, subject to exclusive ownership and archive-identity checks. This does not erase legacy import files. [#159419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159419)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Permission changes and restart recovery">

Changing another user's identity permissions preserves your unrelated connections, accepted chat work, HTTP requests, and pending approvals. Changes to your own effective grants or global access policy still revoke affected work, and restoring grants does not revive it. Revoking Guest access cancels its registered commands and stops private containers dedicated to that guest. [Restart recovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) also checks whether work is still authorized; stopped delegated work may need `/new` or `/reset`, while its transcript remains.

Back up your data before upgrading, including SQLite's write-ahead log. This change upgrades shared state from schema 18 to 19. Downgrading requires a matching pre-upgrade backup in a separate state directory and can lose later permission revocations and receipts. Never lower schema markers to force a downgrade.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Let session-only readers browse authorized conversations and progress, and session writers organize their own chats, without broad Gateway access. Other people's visible chats remain read-only; deletion, sharing, diagnostics, and artifact downloads require separate permissions. [#155184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155184) — @shakkernerd

**Security and trust**

- Require surviving source authority before restart dispatch and retain eligible pending work when transcript reading fails. Existing queue-owned delivery keeps its separate owner. [#150702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150702) — @vincentkoc, @pgondhi987
- Keep private child-session references out of unauthorized event projections while reusing eligible large-message encodings. [#155997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155997)
- Cancel commands after original Guest access is revoked and stop qualifying private containers. Workspace files and writable layers survive; process and temporary-filesystem state do not. Shared, preexisting, mixed-use, and unclassified containers are excluded from whole-container shutdown. [#156106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156106) — @shakkernerd
- Preserve channel-owner revoke-and-restore history across recovery without automatically granting existing links recovery authority. [#157709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157709) — @joshavant
- Require recorded human origin for internal restart recovery, including prepared replies, while retaining separate legacy channel, Control UI, and child-completion checks. Unverified internal work is skipped without dispatch or a recovery notice. [#160130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160130) — @vincentkoc, @romneyda

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve verified WebSocket connections and accepted runs through unrelated identity-permission edits or equivalent scope reordering. Pending handshakes and HTTP/cookie authority retain their existing rules. [#159939](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159939) — @vacinc
- Extend unrelated identity-permission continuity to pending handshakes, token/password and node connections, HTTP requests, plugin cookies, queued work, and approvals. Own-grant and global-policy revocation remain; separate approval invalidation from agent/store inventory edits is outside this fix. [#160136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160136) — @Nakagawa-master

**Limits and compatibility**

If Guest access ends during container setup, inspect and preserve needed data before explicitly recreating the incomplete container. Container shutdown does not cover arbitrary detached descendants in shared or unclassified environments.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="SSH connections and saved credentials">

Paired diagnostics regain their expected access, and [SSH Gateway connections](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/remote) keep using the right saved credential when a forwarding port changes. Existing `sshTarget` setups now manage forwarding automatically unless `transport=direct` is selected. Older URL-only setups need route configuration and one-time token or password enrollment with pairing approval. Use a new SSH alias for a different Gateway, or its saved token could go to the new destination.

Plugin authors must handle credentials becoming unavailable during reload and avoid caching them across reloads. If the managed-secret forwarding worker fails, restart the Gateway to restore forwarding.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Reject late worker SSH identities before host credential preparation after cancellation, replacement, or timeout; provider-internal effects still depend on cooperation with the optional currentness callback. [#150621](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150621) — @sallyom, @vincentkoc
- Stop stale plugin credential reads and self-waiting reload cleanup. Prepared-secret access ends as soon as the exact instance retires, including admitted callbacks; already returned values are not erased and vendor credentials are not revoked. [#153187](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153187) — @jb510, @jalehman, @patrick-erichsen
- Reject whitespace-only Copilot enterprise credential scope during copying, adoption, and shared-owner checks without rewriting malformed stored values. [#160171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160171) — @patrick-erichsen

**Improvements**

- Move managed-secret transfer processing to a dedicated worker while retaining destination restrictions and closing revoked streams. [#156354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156354)
- Reuse TLS trust and protected HTTPS connections within each process grant, with separate pools and revocation for other grants. [#156357](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156357)

**Bug fixes**

- Accept trusted Windows secret-plan directories using structured owner and ACL facts while retaining stricter executable-parent checks. [#158785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158785)
- Restore paired diagnostics and bind managed SSH credentials to the selected route; unpinned manual-loopback diagnostics still need explicit credentials. [#159169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159169) — @iwhatsskill

**Documentation**

- Clarify [trusted IMAP authentication headers](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/imap). Before enabling this trust option, the receiving mail server must strip or replace incoming headers claiming the trusted server identity. The option can admit mail despite local DMARC none or fail; OpenClaw cannot determine the originating hop from the message alone. Runtime behavior and defaults are unchanged. [#160676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160676) — @eleqtrizit

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Workspace files and sandboxes">

Memory reads stay within their authorized folder when retrying, and canceled delegated work stops starting new workspace transfers. Required sandboxing now applies from the first request through the OpenAI-compatible APIs. If the required sandbox is unavailable, the run is refused.

Podman follows your selected connection and SSH identity. If an existing sandbox stops working after upgrading, follow the [Podman recovery guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/sandboxing/podman-backend). Restore its original connection and save needed container data before recreating it. Mounted workspace files remain, but data inside the old container is not transferred.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Enforce role-required sandboxing before the first HTTP session run and reject restricted callers attempting to run existing unsandboxed sessions. [#155964](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155964)
- Keep memory reads inside the original authorized directory when that directory is replaced during a read. [#156222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156222)
- Retain original session sources during asynchronous sandbox reporting and reject changed aliases or databases without granting new permissions. [#156686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156686)
- Recheck run authority around final workspace creation. Revocation detected after an already-started directory operation does not roll back its effects. [#157505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157505)
- Stop guarded later workspace and transport operations after delegated cancellation while preserving independently owned tunnels, accepted transfers, and cleanup. [#159087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159087) — @sallyom, @vincentkoc

**Bug fixes**

- Honor Podman connection precedence and matching SSH identity, with explicit recovery for existing sandboxes and refusal when competing selectors cannot be resolved. [041bbac5](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/041bbac5ca25b471eb7c625d82b485a34f000892) — @vincentkoc
- Reject stale prompt refreshes and skip unused elevation-policy preparation when elevation is disabled. [#157021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157021)
- Refresh security-scan coverage, recheck workspace writes, complete short attachment reads, clean failed cookie snapshots, and preserve symlink chains and literal tilde paths. Scans remain bounded. [#157524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157524)
- Settle failed workspace transfers before returning and honor guarded agent-file metadata checks. [#158313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158313) — @lovelefeng-glitch
- Preserve newline-bearing remote sandbox paths and allowed parent aliases, and accept structured-input choices with padded labels. SSH targets require GNU-compatible `readlink -f` and `-n`. [#159346](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159346)
- Restore creation of new memory files in OpenShell mirror mode by recognizing wrapped missing-file errors, while retaining write provenance and rejecting permission or boundary errors. [#160697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160697) — @sunlit-deng, @jtatum, @obviyus
- Preserve browser upload staging quotas when a queued upload is canceled, and hold memory-write locks until pending rollback, checkpoint, or rewind writes finish. Cancellation still returns promptly; partial write failures are not made atomic. [#160810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160810)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="File permissions during repairs">

[Doctor](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/checks) preserves compatible POSIX file permissions when moving retired `TOOLS.md` notes into `AGENTS.md`. If it cannot do so safely, it warns and keeps both originals. Fix the permissions or merge the notes manually, because agents no longer read `TOOLS.md` at startup. Cleanup of legacy temporary plugin files on macOS and Linux also skips files belonging to another account.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve POSIX owner, group, and mode during compatible tool-note migration, creating missing destinations privately and refusing unsafe imports. This does not establish ACL preservation or Windows equivalence. [#159194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159194)
- Recheck ownership before removing eligible tokenless legacy plugin and model-catalog temporary directories on macOS and Linux. Windows keeps its existing age and rename checks. [#160155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160155) — @romneyda, @vyctorbrzezowski, @jalehman

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Plugin command permissions">

Plugin commands are now rejected if their permission declarations are invalid. Public commands also cannot pass along the private permission used to author skills. Valid plugin commands keep their existing behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Reject invalid plugin command permission scopes, including falsy entries and sparse-array holes. [#158638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158638)
- Strip private skill-authoring capability from public command input at runtime instead of relying on its TypeScript type. [#159220](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159220)

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Duplicate user profiles">

Administrators can [merge duplicate user profiles](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/users), including profiles without email addresses, with `openclaw users merge <source-profile-id> --into <target-profile-id>`. The target keeps its role and conflicting preferences; old permissions do not transfer and historical records keep their original identities. Move personal `USER.md` files manually and expect affected clients to reconnect. The shared Owner profile cannot be merged.

Configured [Cloudflare OIDC sign-in](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloudflare-access) now recognizes GitHub identities in authenticated custom claims. If optional GitHub details are missing or lookup fails, otherwise authorized email sign-in can continue. Existing account conflicts still require an administrator to link the email explicitly with `users.linkEmail`; profiles are not merged automatically.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add explicit administrator-only profile merging with exact live IDs, inherited alias/account transfer rules, safe repeat handling, and rejection of attempts to redirect a retired source. This does not automatically match people or rewrite historical access records. [#159889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159889)

**Bug fixes**

- Read Cloudflare OIDC GitHub claims from `custom` when `oidc_fields` is absent, preserving existing roles, co-author preferences, access requirements, and account-binding checks. A present `oidc_fields` container takes precedence; optional enrichment failures do not grant a GitHub identity or credit. [#160661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160661) — @shakkernerd

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Execution audit identities">

Optional [execution audits](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/audit) now keep track of who started Control UI work through queued replies, retries, and ACP turns. Enable them with `logging.audit.executionIdentity`. Missing or ambiguous identities remain unknown.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Carry authenticated chat identity into execution audits while preserving the original queued identity through profile changes. The in-memory carrier does not restore identity from durable queue state. [#159843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159843) — @joshavant

</details>
</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Quality-of-Life Improvements

You can handle more everyday settings and shared work through chat, with updates to terminal controls and busy conversations.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Settings and skills in chat">

You can ask your agent to change [OpenClaw settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/config) and update skills you own. Existing approvals still apply.

If your settings or tokens contain environment-variable expressions, check the [configuration guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-secrets-env) before updating. Some values now change meaning and can break access.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

Existing `${VAR:-fallback}` text now expands to an environment value or fallback. Use `$${VAR:-fallback}` when you intend literal text, and check existing credentials before updating. These defaults are ordinary configuration text, not secret storage.

Keys saved through chat go into shared team storage. Pasting a key has already exposed it to the model provider and transcript; use masked setup to keep it out of that context.

**Improvements**

- Configure secret references and edit owner-controlled skills through chat [8fd72da](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8fd72da7f2212ce86b77a36d95880b6a6eef8180). Thanks @obviyus.
- Reduce repeated environment scans during configuration changes [#156457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156457). Thanks @vincentkoc.

**Bug fixes**

- Reject unsupported secret-reference targets during configuration previews, retaining plain-value preview limits [#157960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157960). Thanks @olli0103, @obviyus, and @azakharko.
- Make OC Path Markdown writes honor numbered, first, and last item selectors, including the selected duplicate key and positions after multiline edits [#160552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160552).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Add inline environment defaults and literal escaping; existing fallback-shaped credentials require attention [fcfa4bf](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fcfa4bf0c121c30a33d4aa57bc41c1fffa84ba85). Thanks @etzelm and @jalehman.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Connected people and shared sessions">

You can ask your agent [who is connected and which device was recently used](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/presence), within your existing access. Connected tabs may be idle, and any reported location is approximate.

With `operator.write` permission, you can also ask the agent to [archive, restore, or stop sessions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session-tool) you can access and created or were assigned to. Stop normally clears queued follow-ups, so request `clearQueued: false` if you want to keep them.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Query connected people and device activity with the original caller's read authority [#159117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159117).
- Give write operators limited archive, restore, and Stop controls for accessible creator or human-assignee sessions; assignment or `operator.sessions.write` alone is insufficient [#160296](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160296). Thanks @vacinc.

**Documentation**

- Add a [Team-server development walkthrough](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/team-server#how-we-build-openclaw-with-openclaw) covering existing collaboration and PR review, with deployment handled separately [#157675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157675).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Larger Swarms and parallel agents">

[Swarms](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/swarm) now default to 32 collectors per group, up from eight, and no longer compete with ordinary subagents for the same running slots. Ordinary subagent limits also apply separately to each spawning conversation. This can increase resource use; set `tools.swarm.maxConcurrent=8` to keep the previous Swarm cap.

Agents also get guidance to avoid repeatedly asking permission for work you already requested. Existing approvals still apply, and [verified human contributor credit](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/user-model) can follow new delegations with the person's consent.

By default, zero-wait messages to an agent’s own running child now try to guide its current work. Choose `mode: "followup"` when you need a separate turn and completion. Acceptance does not confirm the child has read it, and queued guidance can be lost on restart.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Give each immediate spawning session its own ordinary subagent execution budget [#156381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156381).
- Separate Swarm group capacity from ordinary children and raise the group default to 32 [#158616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158616).
- Clarify bounded research delegation and diagnosis of missing results without restarting cancelled work [#141209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141209). Thanks @hartmark and @jalehman.
- Preserve consenting, verified human coauthor eligibility through delegation, with up to 32 inherited profiles and forward-only credit repair [#157268](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157268). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce repeated confirmations for requested work, update new-workspace guidance, and preserve useful fetched-text previews [#157447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157447). Thanks @obviyus, @jalehman, and @joshavant.
- Reduce history scanning during subagent capacity checks without changing which children count [#155695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155695). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Expose the existing zero-to-60-second wait range, 30-second default, and immediate-snapshot option to models [#156542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156542).
- Avoid whole-store reloads for active-subagent context and selected lists [#158397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158397).
- Avoid unrelated registry work when waiting for known collectors [51ec968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/51ec96836f768c07c80e9d11af872014e7436d69). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Update subagent list indexes when displayed facts change [#158792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158792).
- Reduce repeated recovery reads, with unresolved rows backing off from one second to one minute [#159082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159082).
- Move durable requester lookup during native, visible, and ACP spawning to database workers [#159135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159135).
- Replace existing subagent registry entries without scanning unrelated siblings; the position indexes add some resident memory, and deletion still shifts later entries [#160800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160800).
- Move native completion-binding and ACP notice lookups off the Gateway thread [#157033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157033).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Historical subagent Task-ledger completion writes moved to workers before the Tasks retirement; this is not a retained Task-ledger capability [#156467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156467).
- Default `sessions_send` calls with `timeoutSeconds: 0` and no mode try steering the caller’s own active child; idle children and rejected steering fall through to a new turn on the same child. Accepted steering skips separate announcement delivery and does not confirm transcript persistence or model consumption; explicit modes, positive waits, and other targets retain their behavior [#160842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160842). Thanks @CarotaWealth.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Unfinished checklist reminders">

Before ending a reply, the built-in agent gets one chance to revisit an unfinished [checklist it saved during that run](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/progress-card). It can continue the work or explain a blocker, which may use another model response. This does not restart old tasks or override cancellation and approval waits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Add one current-run completion check for successfully saved unfinished checklists, preserving visible checkpoints and normal stopping exceptions [#160297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160297). Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Terminal models and conversations">

The [terminal model picker](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/tui) opens while choices load and keeps your search as it refreshes. Startup and session refreshes also preserve the conversation you selected. Text submitted while a conversation loads stays editable until you press Enter again; a startup `--message` that was not sent is reported and is not retried automatically.

Selecting bare `global` now opens Home, including saved selections, while older history remains stored. Terminal math display and Tab completion for Chinese, Japanese, and Korean attachment names also improve.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Open model pickers immediately and refresh choices while preserving search and selection [#157839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157839). Thanks @obviyus.
- Use the consistent `openclaw` process title for ordinary CLI commands [#157118](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157118).
- Reuse pinned configuration during proxy validation [#158071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158071).
- Keep inference help and completion free of execution-only runtime loading [#158624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158624).
- Improve terminal math display and explicit Tab completion for CJK attachment names; automatic completion triggers stay unchanged [#160085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160085).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep session descriptions and remembered-session restoration with the selected agent [#158240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158240).
- Preserve choices made during startup restore and join accepted remembered-session writes on normal exit [#158845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158845).
- Keep history and sends on the selected conversation, falling back from qualified global to Home only after confirmed absence [#159166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159166).
- Correct image and video timeout guidance to use `--timeout-ms` [#157419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157419). Thanks @obviyus and @sxh313; the source also records the name sxh without establishing another person.
- Exit after plugin-sensitive help output has drained [#158024](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158024). Thanks @obviyus and @ryanjkelly.
- Return parseable JSON errors for rejected agent `/compact` messages, pointing to `sessions compact` [#159212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159212).
- Tell operators to review a mismatched conditional config expectation rather than repeat the same command; expectation flags still cannot combine with `--dry-run` [#159708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159708).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Let CLI commands exit during compile-cache cleanup; retention targets remain soft and Node permission mode skips maintenance [#157884](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157884). Thanks @romneyda.
- Reduce eligible Linux broker and service-anchor launch overhead; missing executables on that path now report `ENOENT` instead of shell status 127 [#158367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158367).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Code Mode activity and errors">

[Code Mode](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/code-mode/tool-surface) shows a purpose title for new executions, and its [error reports](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/code-mode/troubleshooting) distinguish a failed tool from a later JavaScript error. A failed script may already have sent a message or taken another action, so check before retrying. Optional [loop detection](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/loop-detection) also recognizes more repeated work and remains off by default.

Shell commands can use more of the current Code Mode call’s remaining time to finish, reducing unnecessary follow-up checks for short commands. Longer commands still return a process handle, and explicit background requests keep their behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Require a nonblank title for new Code Mode calls while retaining older untitled history [#157150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157150).
- Reduce Node execution overhead and retain bounded output when a worker times out [#156066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156066).
- Extend bounded warm-worker reuse and expose per-script start and retirement counters [#156387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156387).
- Reuse up to four compatible completed Code Mode workers, each with five-minute idle retention [#156800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156800).
- Reduce temporary allocations in OpenClaw Tool Search catalog preparation [#158805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158805).
- Reuse search terms for unchanged tool descriptions across Code Mode cells while refreshing changed entries and keeping visibility-specific results [#160811](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160811).

**Bug fixes**

- Skip spoken confirmation for supported read-only Talk file counts, listings, and composed commands whose every stage is read-only [#156151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156151). Thanks @obviyus, @peetiegonzalez, @vacinc, and @marvinthebored.
- Explain when disabled exec exit notifications require polling or another continuation for needed results [#158884](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158884). Thanks @vacinc.
- Preserve explanatory text for supported explicit nested tool-error shapes [#159359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159359).
- Avoid logging-import memory exhaustion before Code Mode programs start [#159615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159615).
- Recognize repeated Code Mode work despite changing execution bookkeeping when loop detection is enabled [#159864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159864). Thanks @vacinc.
- Distinguish JavaScript errors from original tool rejections across execution and waits, independently of replay safety [#160217](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160217). Thanks @vacinc.
- Give default shell commands the remaining Code Mode wait window minus a settlement margin instead of a one-second cap; explicit timing stays in effect, and slow settlement may still require another wait [#160841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160841).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Uploaded documents and PDF pages">

The assistant keeps the filenames of uploaded documents and is told when extraction leaves out known pages, text, or images. [PDF page selections](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/pdf) now count the pages you request, so a small page limit no longer rules out a page near the end of a file. Recognized binary documents also return reading or conversion guidance instead of garbled text.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Report observed partial extraction and apply PDF limits to the number of selected pages [2a5cda2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2a5cda2715be235e41824251ec22154f21aa0f1f). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Recognize binary document bytes and return parser or conversion guidance, without adding automatic extraction [5cc0d5b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5cc0d5b8a0a41c7de40876becd662ace63b2a7b5). Thanks @vincentkoc and reporter @xx77yy.
- Preserve uploaded filenames through storage and sandbox staging into model context [#156696](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156696).
- Preserve emoji and following embedding text under tiny byte caps, allowing one indivisible character to exceed the cap; stop cancelled browser permission-upgrade waits and reconnects while finishing storage of already-approved rotated credentials [#158571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158571).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Release notes and documentation">

Release pages and plain-Markdown copies link to each other, so you can choose the format you prefer. Documentation navigation includes existing Persian and Thai translations, and old docs Showcase links point to the [website Showcase](https://openclaw.ai/showcase). Contributors can also preview edits with the current website renderer.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Documentation**

- Add the prior release's plain-Markdown changelog and contribution record [#156875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156875). Thanks @hannesrudolph.
- Give formatted release pages and Markdown mirrors reciprocal reading-format links [#156916](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156916). Thanks @hannesrudolph.
- Use current-site local previews and include Persian and Thai in publishing navigation; Windows preview handoff remains unverified [#157544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157544). Thanks @hannesrudolph.
- Retire the stale docs Showcase and configure old bookmarks to reach the existing website destination [#158575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158575). Thanks @hannesrudolph.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Managed workspace setup and cleanup">

[Managed workspaces](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) skip unnecessary setup for empty checkouts and repeated cleanup scans of unchanged checkouts that cannot be removed. If you repair one outside OpenClaw, run `openclaw worktrees gc` to retry cleanup; otherwise it may remain deferred until OpenClaw records further activity or a lifecycle change.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Batch file-metadata checks when preparing large worktree snapshots [6fce79b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6fce79bffd93a99a13f76d06983ad941c0426543). Credit to Peter Steinberger.
- Save workspace safety records through the state worker while preserving durable timestamps and the 24-hour disappearance guard [#158797](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158797).
- Move managed-worktree run admission off the Gateway thread, retaining uncertain-outcome custody until recovery [#159437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159437).
- Skip template creation for known-empty workspace trees while retaining normal checkout and capacity checks [#159544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159544).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Remember worktree cleanup deferrals and reduce ordinary PR-polling Git work; external repairs require an explicit cleanup retry [#158892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158892).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Conversation history and lists">

Opening conversation history and refreshing session lists require fewer repeated checks and reads. Lists also avoid unpacking large saved snapshots they do not need, and browsing a parent’s child conversations skips unrelated sessions. Large session-group changes do less background work, though the effect on loading time depends on your setup.

Back up your databases before updating and verify the backup. This update automatically moves agent databases to [schema 24](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/agent-schema-history), which older builds cannot open. To downgrade, restore a verified pre-upgrade backup with its matching build; do not lower database version markers.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse unchanged session placement information after transcript writes [08e5e35](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/08e5e350d35e88f091040357d5ac6367fdefcecb).
- Reuse completed placement reads during overlapping archived-session preparation [63fa1d2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/63fa1d2dedac5eb6ffa5ec690457d41e8ac22cd0). Credit to Peter Steinberger.
- Separate large saved snapshots from frequently read session metadata in agent schema 24, preserving full reads and transcript bytes. Reported allocation reductions apply to isolated metadata-read fixtures, not total Gateway memory [ae6a884](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ae6a88438de360932c02007849e6727fa6fe1848).
- Narrow eligible child-session lists through existing parent indexes while preserving visibility, ownership, counts, and paging; person-profile searches retain the full visible roster [#160404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160404).
- Reuse authentication readers across connection and scheduled-work callbacks [#155779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155779).
- Prepare operator profiles away from the Gateway request thread [#155800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155800).
- Retain prepared session rows across refresh pauses [#155957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155957). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Avoid rebuilding rosters for progress and preview updates [#155995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155995).
- Reuse prepared session information when loading conversation history [#156055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156055).
- Reuse roster selection within session-list preparation [#156221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156221).
- Reuse admitted database schema facts during session reads [#156643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156643).
- Share overlapping same-row session reads during refresh [#156791](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156791).
- Remove repeated schema probes from warm session metadata reads [#156817](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156817).
- Share freshness checks within one synchronous read while later independent reads recheck for foreign commits [#156922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156922).
- Avoid repeated schema inspection after data-only writes [#157030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157030).
- Share pending validation across concurrent session readers [#157043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157043).
- Avoid unrelated row-validation waits for targeted access after store admission [#157113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157113).
- Avoid copying the process environment for resident subagent identity checks [#157120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157120).
- Reuse session facts after model metadata changes [#157129](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157129).
- Reuse unchanged related rows during parent and child relationship refreshes [#157552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157552).
- Reuse current participant facts when publishing session writes [#157582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157582).
- Reuse current participant facts during warm transcript appends [#157622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157622).
- Reduce temporary filtering arrays during session status reads [#158326](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158326).
- Batch exact reads for session-group changes involving at least ten members [#158345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158345).
- Preserve unrelated placement facts after participant updates [#158395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158395).
- Avoid repeated path normalization during warm session reads [#158444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158444).
- Reuse prepared database-admission facts during session updates [#158627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158627).
- Reuse resident session facts during mutation checks [#158833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158833).
- Reuse resident exact rows during tool-policy preparation [#158872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158872).
- Reuse healthy cleanup workers after settled commit refusals without replaying refused work [#158878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158878).
- Reduce cleanup planning work and time occupying the writer [#158885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158885).
- Decode exact session rows once for both use and validation [#158961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158961).
- Avoid schema rescans caused by mistaking SQL expression endings for transaction boundaries [#158973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158973).
- Route run events through existing session identities instead of repeatedly scanning full stores [#159742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159742).
- Reduce duplicate ownership checks within uninterrupted state-read work [#159876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159876).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Reuse dedicated-read ownership proof for less than one second, including after an out-of-band lock-sidecar replacement or directory-alias change; expired proof is checked before dispatch [#160139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160139).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Saving conversations">

More conversation saving can wait for storage without holding up unrelated requests. Large messages and tool results also need less duplicate processing when saved, although individual saves can still take longer.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move selected session writes off the request thread and let foreground writes progress between cleanup units [#155631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155631).
- Move durable history and selected session-detail reads off the request thread [#155740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155740).
- Reuse valid integrity proof across same-process database workers while retaining conservative cold-open checks [#155785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155785).
- Move streamed transcript and runtime-report writes to workers while preserving commit ordering [#156095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156095).
- Reduce large-message storage preparation without changing transcript bytes [#158402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158402).
- Reduce host-side preparation for SQLite worker operations [#158655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158655).
- Reuse warm agent-database execution context and reduce repeated host checks [#158750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158750).
- Create fresh canonical durable sessions with one atomic transcript-header and entry write; alias, fork, and Incognito paths remain distinct [#158771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158771).
- Reduce host CPU spent publishing small agent-state changes [#158843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158843).
- Avoid transferring duplicate parsed tool calls and results to the database worker [#159449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159449).
- Copy prior session-update context only when a callback reads it [#159780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159780).
- Avoid unpacking unrelated large tool results during guarded writes and prior-answer matching, with clearer write diagnostics [#159898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159898).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Starting replies in parallel">

Independent conversations can prepare replies at the same time through more paths, reducing waits behind other chats. Starting a new conversation also avoids unnecessary history scans. Busy setups can still experience pauses.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Remove redundant session-metadata reads during chat admission [7dc4905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7dc4905d61a8544001c84c8794c6b24e985fd7d5).
- Move conversation-index publication and selected startup reads into workers [e0d8000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e0d80000067d224e93af6107ed093a21e046879f). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev and @joshavant.
- Move managed-worktree snapshot reads off the calling thread [#156315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156315). Thanks @todddickerson.
- Share simultaneous approval-replay preparation for matching session and reviewer bindings, retaining per-client checks and bounded retries [#156358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156358).
- Batch worker-machine metadata refreshes through background database reads [#157079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157079).
- Skip device-storage setup for eligible implicit local loopback CLI calls with authentication disabled [#157166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157166).
- Read opt-in CLI timeline settings without opening shared state [#157252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157252).
- Reuse healthy discovery workers during session creation and expose slow-create phase diagnostics [#158433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158433).
- Avoid empty archive-recovery waits before replies when the committed database identity still matches [#158559](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158559).
- Reuse sandbox classification and avoid duplicate workspace-directory preparation [#158610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158610).
- Skip unnecessary archive preparation when adopting existing sessions [#158660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158660).
- Read targeted rows and label occupancy instead of unrelated session inventory during conversation creation [#158809](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158809).
- Prepare personal-account authority once for concurrent chat work; running installations require profile changes through Gateway RPCs [#158941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158941).
- Yield between session-publication batches and combine pending activity recaps within the same session generation [#159102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159102).
- Allow audited operations on independent session keys to prepare replies concurrently while overlapping operations keep their ordering [#160183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160183).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Historical Task-owner startup persistence moved off the Gateway thread before the Tasks system was retired [#156064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156064).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Live replies and progress updates">

OpenClaw does less repeated processing while displaying replies and sharing progress updates. Very large updates also avoid extra work once they exceed what can be replayed after a reconnect.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip full runtime-context cleanup scans when recognized removable headers are absent [#153211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153211). Thanks @jjjhenriksen, @patrick-erichsen, and @noelillinger.
- Stop capturing oversized progress at the existing replay limit and retain compact tool metadata [#158572](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158572).
- Reuse unchanged tool-definition projections while applying current diagnostic redaction [#158871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158871).
- Reduce repeated identity resolution and allocation in session-activity broadcasts [#159182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159182).
- Encode matching multi-client broadcasts once while retaining JSON text frames and recipient restrictions; retained delivery and recipient-specific projections keep their existing paths [#160787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160787).
- Reuse exact measurements of unchanged long text within an agent’s diagnostic recording attempt, while observing edits and compaction and leaving provider requests unchanged [#160807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160807).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Memory use during busy chats">

Busy chats release large pieces of data they no longer need. Related memory-management changes reduced long pauses in tests with many simultaneous replies, though pauses can still occur.

Closing Control UI terminal tabs also releases memory that could remain held after the tab closed, while other open terminals keep working.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move managed-worktree lease cleanup off the Gateway thread; the accompanying Task-backed maintenance settlement is historical after Tasks retirement [#156640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156640).
- Prepare worktree cleanup workers earlier; the accompanying Task-backed maintenance-start transition was subsequently retired [#156872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156872).
- Retain one idle agent-database executor for up to thirty minutes, with cross-agent eviction unchanged [#158405](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158405).
- Release unneeded large event payloads retained by live-chat ownership checks [#158643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158643).
- Reduce plugin ownership bookkeeping behind long garbage-collection pauses during concurrent streaming [#159103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159103).
- Reduce repeated tool-classification allocation during agent preparation [#159464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159464).

**Bug fixes**

- Remove the listener retaining closed Control UI terminals’ canvas and WebAssembly memory. Browser tests cover this specific leak, without establishing recovery from native-app renderer crashes [#160748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160748). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Other Bug Fixes

This release fixes several causes of stalled conversations, missing results, and interrupted worker chats.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Database and conversation history">

Conversation lists keep refreshing while other chats save changes. The release also fixes temporary-file buildup that could block updates despite free disk space, and lets an agent accept new requests after a temporary database cleanup failure clears. Failed requests are not automatically repeated.

Busy installations may use more memory for queued database input, with its allowance rising from 64 to 256 MiB. If a `global` or `unknown` session address is rejected as ambiguous, choose a specific conversation. Existing history is kept without merging or renaming it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep archived-session search, terminal history, Talk, and shared PR updates current. [0bbad56f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0bbad56fa80945db4323771873c9944e67454679) Thanks @galiniliev, @steipete.
- Keep history reads working during concurrent agent registration. [3d903819](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d903819c5641ed8735aebe55c86844957009952) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Avoid database-worker contention during Gateway authentication recovery. [3f341061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3f341061594210e824b05aa20826af23ec0c5c76) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Preserve concurrent session metadata while storage becomes ready. [526f67dc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/526f67dc6382bae28cb26231b0813ce755521d80) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Release memory-pressure listeners after database-worker cleanup. [8a5386d6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8a5386d63e0472ad081c0533badde6a5a174394a) Thanks @steipete.
- Prevent temporary-file exhaustion from SQLite coordination, retain Cron result writes during shutdown, and stop retired Wear audio frames changing the current animation. [8b16e532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8b16e5328ecde96f7fe48aa637fec18cfde3fd47) Thanks @steipete.
- Explain when a database path disappears during access checks, without treating the file as recovered. [9512a28b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9512a28b6fe6520a5377d38a287494b70cf2e55a) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Release database access after a command reaches a closed worker. [a53728bd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a53728bd3b61b38049cb875103ed455cc44e87fe) Thanks David Stephens.
- Preserve stored session addresses, history, and family links through alias changes; reject stale queued writes. [#155886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155886) Thanks @steipete.
- Preserve saved sessions, ACP metadata, and existing literal parent links when the main-session alias changes; absent parents retain the existing alias fallback. [#156109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156109) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Stop counting a completed request as active while background participant details are saved. [#156172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156172) Thanks @steipete.
- End refresh loops for absent subagent runs and reuse unchanged run information. [#156268](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156268) Thanks @steipete.
- Keep session lists refreshing while unrelated sessions are being written. [#156286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156286) Thanks @steipete.
- Avoid rebuilding unchanged session lists after identity-permission changes. [#156339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156339) Thanks @steipete.
- Reject session and Cron metadata invalidated while their database reader closes. [#156519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156519) Thanks @steipete.
- Restore sharing and transcript notes for custom session-store locators backed by symlinked databases. [#156684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156684) Thanks @steipete.
- Show another connection's committed database changes on the next independent read, preserving an active read snapshot. [#156824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156824) Thanks @vincentkoc, @steipete.
- Keep a healthy SQLite connection usable after a pre-commit automatic rollback; the failed write still fails. [#157241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157241) Thanks @steipete.
- Save durable session activity through the database worker, preserving event order and stopping replay when a write's outcome is unknown; the first flush may take longer, and Incognito retains its existing handling. [#157598](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157598) Thanks @steipete.
- Keep older child-answer lookups and selected Cron database waits off the Gateway thread; accompanying Tasks and TaskFlow read paths were later retired. [#157634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157634) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Reuse valid same-process database integrity checks after the last writer closes. [#157666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157666) Thanks @marinabotobs, @steipete.
- Attempt tool-resource cleanup when agent setup fails, preserving the original error and reporting uncertain cleanup. [#157678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157678) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Resolve dangling workspace symlinks correctly during agent deletion and close File Transfer handles after identity-check errors; terminal uploads now remove some forbidden filename characters and use names such as `CON_.txt`. [#158008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158008) Thanks @steipete.
- Let Gateway requests proceed while session cleanup and cold-storage commits settle. [#158073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158073) Thanks @steipete.
- Prevent the reply-startup lock cycle caused by overlapping read-marker updates with a durable context engine. [#158209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158209) Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Skip malformed non-object Claude CLI history rows and retain the original abort error for already-aborted node work. [#158228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158228) Thanks @steipete.
- Avoid chat-send lock waits when the needed device identity is already cached. [#158259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158259) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Allow session creation through database directory aliases such as macOS /tmp. [#158401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158401) Thanks @joshavant.
- Finish releasing a failed-startup turn claim after temporary database contention, allowing the next message without replaying the failed input. [#158486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158486) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev, @steipete.
- Share agent database access across directory aliases and reject queued writes after an alias is retargeted. [#158611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158611) Thanks @joshavant.
- Release completed-run diagnostic references after queued events drain. [#158949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158949) Thanks @steipete.
- Allow the next request after a transient failed-operation database-cleanup fault clears, while explicit revocation remains terminal. [#159451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159451) Thanks @aspalagin and @obviyus; related issue report by @desksk.
- Keep persistent reply startup and Stop responsive during database admission waits, joining cleanup before a queued successor. [#159632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159632) Thanks @steipete.
- Restore the loaded conversation after a rejected nested transcript write, preserving selected branches and valid message ancestry. [#159807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159807) Thanks @steipete.
- Let other Gateway work continue while a session update waits to begin its database write; the transaction itself remains synchronous. [#160801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160801) Thanks @steipete.
- Retain the normal bounded commit wait after write admission, so brief competing reads do not immediately fail session updates on rollback-journal storage. Commit waits remain synchronous. [#160887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160887) Thanks @steipete.
- Reject asynchronous transcript messages carrying stale writer or session-lifecycle claims before saving them. [#160893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160893) Thanks @steipete.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Earlier asynchronous Tasks retention cleanup, superseded by the removal of Tasks and TaskFlow. [ac9b848d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ac9b848d2f0bb7126759d06046727cf1d24e4d5d) Thanks @steipete.
- Keep admitted chat writes attached to their original conversation and database; refuse ambiguous raw session aliases without merging history. [#156819](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156819) Thanks @steipete.
- Give independent database workers separate request slots and raise their shared queued-input allowance from 64 to 256 MiB. [#157230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157230) Thanks @steipete; issue report by @todddickerson.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Subagent results and restarts">

A finished [subagent batch](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/announce) can now return its results while an unrelated earlier batch keeps working. It still waits for its own helpers and their nested tasks. The release also fixes cases where helper results restarted work you had stopped, or an interrupted task could not find its original request after a restart. An agent resuming after its helpers finish can also keep using its authorized tools.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve successful child completion timing when publishing its reply. [53f7e5fd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/53f7e5fd46f5ac44b9d690549b73def4d7b526ba) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Reserve collector queue order before requester lookup and retain the original completion destination. [5cf18de3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5cf18de3d103a6bea797cbfe52fa8f51dce8c596) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Resume an agent after a clean sessions_yield instead of mistaking a stale synthetic message for cancellation. [66791fc1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/66791fc10e964525496b22be50013e694e51ffe2) Thanks @steipete.
- Finish failed-launch cancellation before deleting a collector session or advancing its queue, including restored queues. [d00524c2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d00524c295776a673d1450ed44b95021976539e3) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Report cancellation before cleaning up a collector stopped during startup. [d65bc76d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d65bc76d60daaa16078748bd2f2a9c090564d2ee) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Wait for the original replayable reply before agent.wait reports completion; replay remains limited to the existing process-local cache. [#156078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156078) Thanks @joshavant.
- Prevent selected completed helpers from automatically restarting stopped work, retaining their results and allowing new human instructions. [#156124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156124) Thanks @shakkernerd.
- Release a failed restart-recovery attempt so the conversation can retry within its existing recovery budget. [#157214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157214) Thanks @steipete.
- Preserve completion input through compaction and stop futile retries for the specific closed-turn transcript mismatch. [#157571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157571) Thanks @steipete.
- Limit repeated identical settlement-write warnings while keeping the existing retry cadence and reporting suppressed counts. [#157871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157871) Thanks @etzelm, @amesgit, @jalehman.
- Record native subagent and ACP terminal outcomes without blocking unrelated Gateway events during database contention. [#158118](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158118) Thanks @steipete.
- Recover an interrupted request and saved tool results when large internal records obscure the loaded history window. [#158363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158363) Thanks @jalehman; related issue reports by @hannesrudolph and @simonspid.
- Refresh optional parent-session attribution after creation, replacement, or deletion. [#158581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158581) Thanks @steipete.
- Preserve a successful nested subagent result after restart when its exact pending continuation still owns the work. [#158585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158585) Thanks @jalehman.
- Await orphaned-subagent database admission without blocking startup's main thread on integrity scans. [#158620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158620) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Deliver a finished yielded batch without waiting for unrelated older work, retaining waits for its own nested children and cleanup. [#158642](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158642) Thanks @obviyus; report by @potterdigital and independent diagnosis by @sxh313.
- Avoid waiting again for results already being processed, show scheduled agents only permitted automation actions, and tolerate harmless session updates during publication-permission preparation. [#160036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160036) Thanks @steipete.
- Move database work for stopping active or queued file-backed native subagents to workers, preserving the intended child and waiting for completion writes before follow-ups. [#160203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160203) Thanks @steipete; integration work by @vincentkoc and @Kimiyu-186.
- Restore a parent's verified tool policy when it resumes after its helpers finish; missing or conflicting policies keep the restricted fallback, and revoked access stays revoked. [#160786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160786) Thanks @joshavant; related issue report by @andersonjeccel.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Earlier missing-Task completion retirement and inspection, superseded by Tasks removal; this historical change discarded delivery rather than restoring it. [#157061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157061) Thanks @steipete.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Queued messages and steering">

Canceling a [queued message](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) before it starts now leaves the current reply and goal running. Follow-ups sent after a reply finishes go to the next turn, and recovery from stuck loops can let waiting messages proceed. [Steering](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session-tool) sent to a busy built-in agent stays queued, but its acceptance does not mean it has been read or saved. Queued steering is lost on restart.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Route late follow-ups to the next turn after completion or handoff, while current-turn retries can still accept steering. [2fd61364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2fd61364420671efcd6b6c1d57bbd6e59b561863) Thanks @steipete.
